File: test_exact_payload.exs

# test_exact_payload.exs
require Logger

Mix.Task.run("app.start")

# Use raw HTTPoison/Req to send the EXACT payload from the user request
url = "http://localhost:4000/api/v1/ingest"
headers = [
  {"Content-Type", "application/json"},
  {"x-agent-id", "agent_name"}
]

body = %{
  "fact_text" => "The primary declarative memory statement.",
  "agent_id" => "agent_name",
  "acls" => %{
    "read" => ["public", "group_a"],
    "write" => ["root"]
  },
  "provenance" => %{
    "source" => "manual_entry",
    "trust_score" => 0.9
  },
  "description_payloads" => [
    %{ "type" => "text", "content" => "Supporting detail..." },
    %{ "type" => "code", "language" => "elixir", "content" => "def run, do: :ok" },
    %{ "type" => "number", "value" => 42, "unit" => "count" },
    %{ "type" => "link", "uri" => "http://example.com", "description" => "Ref" },
    %{ "type" => "object", "object_type" => "sensor_data", "data" => %{ "temp" => 23 } }
  ]
}

IO.puts "Sending exact payload from spec..."
case Req.post(url, json: body, headers: headers) do
  {:ok, %Req.Response{status: status, body: response_body}} ->
    IO.puts "Status: #{status}"
    IO.inspect(response_body)
  {:error, reason} ->
    IO.puts "Error: #{inspect reason}"
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: test_acms_api.exs
------------------------------------------------------------
# test_acms_api.exs
require Logger

Mix.Task.run("app.start")

alias ACN.Infrastructure.MemoryClient
alias ACN.Core.Artifacts.DBC

IO.puts "--- Testing ACMS Ingestion ---"
dbc = %DBC{
  id: UUID.uuid4(),
  fact_text: "The primary decorative memory statement for testing.",
  author_id: "test_bot",
  timestamp: DateTime.utc_now(),
  verifier_id: "test_verifier",
  artifact_hash: "abc123hash",
  provenance_data: %{
    "source" => "manual_test",
    "trust_score" => 0.95
  }
}

case MemoryClient.ingest(dbc) do
  {:ok, node_id} ->
    IO.puts "✓ Ingestion successful! Node ID: #{node_id}"
  {:error, reason} ->
    IO.puts "✗ Ingestion failed: #{inspect reason}"
end

IO.puts "\n--- Testing ACMS Query ---"
case MemoryClient.query("decorative memory", 5) do
  {:ok, results} ->
    IO.puts "✓ Query successful! Found #{length(results)} results."
    Enum.each(results, fn r ->
      IO.puts "  - [Score: #{Float.round(r.score, 2)}] #{r.fact_text}"
    end)
  {:error, reason} ->
    IO.puts "✗ Query failed: #{inspect reason}"
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: test_panopticon.exs
------------------------------------------------------------
# Start the application
Application.ensure_all_started(:acn)

# Wait a moment for GenServers to boot
Process.sleep(500)

IO.puts("\n=== 1. Starting Panopticon State ===")
IO.inspect(ACN.Observability.Panopticon.snapshot())

IO.puts("\n=== 2. Simulating Task Manager Plan ===")
ACN.Observability.Panopticon.inject_intent("root_tm", 0, [
  %{id: "child_1", task: "Setup db"},
  %{id: "child_2", task: "Write API"}
])

IO.puts("\n=== 3. Simulating Telemetry Events ===")
phase_1_dbc = %ACN.Observability.MicroDBC{
  event_id: :crypto.strong_rand_bytes(16) |> Base.encode16(),
  node_id: "child_1",
  mat_path: "root.db",
  phase_from: :architecting,
  phase_to: :execution,
  hash: "hash123",
  autonomic: %{stress_level: 10.0}
}

phase_2_dbc = %ACN.Observability.MicroDBC{
  event_id: :crypto.strong_rand_bytes(16) |> Base.encode16(),
  node_id: "child_1",
  mat_path: "root.db",
  phase_from: :execution,
  phase_to: :completed,
  prev_hash: "hash123",
  hash: "hash456",
  autonomic: %{stress_level: 5.0}
}

Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(ACN.PubSub, "acn:telemetry:all", {:telemetry_event, phase_1_dbc})
Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(ACN.PubSub, "acn:telemetry:all", {:telemetry_event, phase_2_dbc})

# Give Panopticon a moment to process the async casts/infos
Process.sleep(500)

IO.puts("\n=== 4. Final Panopticon State ===")
final_state = ACN.Observability.Panopticon.snapshot()
IO.inspect(final_state)

if length(final_state.vertices) > 0 do
  IO.puts("\n[SUCCESS] Graph populated with #{length(final_state.vertices)} vertices and #{length(final_state.edges)} edges.")
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: test_hsg_pipeline.exs
------------------------------------------------------------
# Start the application
Application.ensure_all_started(:acn)

IO.puts("\n=== 1. Starting Panopticon State ===")
IO.inspect(ACN.Observability.Panopticon.snapshot())

IO.puts("\n=== 2. Triggering Genesis (Creates root node & starts flow) ===")
# Generate a simple task that forces at least one phase transition
task = "Write a hello world python script in /tmp/hello.py"

case ACN.Genesis.ignite(task) do
  {:ok, _pid} -> 
    IO.puts("Genesis ignited. Waiting for trace events to propagate...")
    # Give the agents a moment to transition phases, run the LLM, and emit HSG events
    Process.sleep(5000)
    
  error -> 
    IO.puts("Failed to ignite: #{inspect(error)}")
end

IO.puts("\n=== 3. Final Panopticon State ===")
# We should now see vertices (agents, hashes) and edges (transitions)
snapshot = ACN.Observability.Panopticon.snapshot()
IO.inspect(snapshot)

# Check if it's still empty
if Enum.empty?(snapshot.vertices) do
  IO.puts("\n[ERROR] Graph is still empty. Emitter hooks might not be firing properly.")
else
  IO.puts("\n[SUCCESS] Graph populated with #{length(snapshot.vertices)} vertices and #{length(snapshot.edges)} edges.")
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: audit.py
------------------------------------------------------------
import pdfplumber
import re
import csv

def extract_high_value_deposits(pdf_path, output_csv_path, threshold=200000.00):
    """
    Parses a bank statement PDF and extracts transactions where the 
    Settlement Credit is >= the specified threshold.
    """
    
    # Regular expression to match the transaction rows based on the document's structure
    # It looks for "COMPLETED", parses references, N/A, and the specific numeric columns.
    tx_pattern = re.compile(
        r'(?P<status>COMPLETED|FAILED|PENDING)\s+'         # Transaction Status
        r'(?:(?P<term_id>[A-Z0-9]{8})\s+)?'               # Terminal ID (Optional)
        r'(?:(?P<rrn>\d{12,})\s+)?'                       # RRN (Optional)
        r'(?P<ref>[A-Za-z0-9_|\-]+)\s+'                   # Transaction Ref
        r'(?P<reversal>N/A|YES|NO)\s+'                    # Reversal Status
        r'(?P<amount>[\d,]+\.\d{2})\s+'                   # Transaction Amount
        r'(?P<debit>[\d,]+\.\d{2})\s+'                    # Settlement Debit
        r'(?P<credit>[\d,]+\.\d{2})\s+'                   # Settlement Credit  <-- TARGET
        r'(?P<bal_before>[\d,]+\.\d{2})\s+'               # Balance Before
        r'(?P<bal_after>[\d,]+\.\d{2})\s*'                # Balance After
        r'(?:(?P<charge>[\d,]+\.\d{2})\s+)?'              # Charge (Optional)
        r'(?P<remainder>.*)'                              # Beneficiary, Source, etc.
    )

    extracted_transactions = []

    print(f"Opening '{pdf_path}'...")
    try:
        with pdfplumber.open(pdf_path) as pdf:
            for page_num, page in enumerate(pdf.pages, start=1):
                # Extract text preserving horizontal layout to keep rows intact
                text = page.extract_text(layout=True)
                if not text:
                    continue
                
                lines = text.split('\n')
                
                for i, line in enumerate(lines):
                    match = tx_pattern.search(line)
                    if match:
                        data = match.groupdict()
                        
                        # Clean the Settlement Credit string and convert to float
                        credit_str = data['credit'].replace(',', '')
                        try:
                            credit_val = float(credit_str)
                        except ValueError:
                            continue
                        
                        # Filter for Deposits >= threshold (200,000)
                        if credit_val >= threshold:
                            
                            # Attempt to grab the next few lines for narration/details
                            # (As narration often spills onto the lines below)
                            narration = data['remainder'].strip()
                            for next_line in lines[i+1:i+3]:
                                # If the next line looks like a new transaction, stop looking
                                if "COMPLETED" in next_line or "AP_TRSF" in next_line:
                                    break
                                if next_line.strip():
                                    narration += " " + next_line.strip()

                            extracted_transactions.append({
                                'Page Number': page_num,
                                'Transaction Ref': data['ref'],
                                'Status': data['status'],
                                'Transaction Amount': data['amount'],
                                'Settlement Debit': data['debit'],
                                'Settlement Credit': data['credit'],
                                'Balance Before': data['bal_before'],
                                'Balance After': data['bal_after'],
                                'Additional Details & Narration': narration
                            })

    except FileNotFoundError:
        print(f"Error: The file '{pdf_path}' was not found. Please make sure it is in the same folder as this script.")
        return
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error reading PDF: {e}")
        return

    # Write results to CSV
    if extracted_transactions:
        print(f"Found {len(extracted_transactions)} transactions with deposits >= {threshold:,.2f}.")
        print(f"Writing to '{output_csv_path}'...")
        
        headers = [
            'Page Number', 'Transaction Ref', 'Status', 'Transaction Amount', 
            'Settlement Debit', 'Settlement Credit', 'Balance Before', 
            'Balance After', 'Additional Details & Narration'
        ]
        
        with open(output_csv_path, mode='w', newline='', encoding='utf-8') as file:
            writer = csv.DictWriter(file, fieldnames=headers)
            writer.writeheader()
            for tx in extracted_transactions:
                writer.writerow(tx)
                
        print("Done! Check your folder for the output.csv file.")
    else:
        print(f"No transactions found with a deposit of {threshold:,.2f} or above.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Updated file names as requested
    INPUT_PDF = "file.pdf" 
    OUTPUT_CSV = "output.csv"
    
    extract_high_value_deposits(INPUT_PDF, OUTPUT_CSV, threshold=200000.00)

# Clean Mnesia Schema script
require Logger

Logger.info("Stopping Mnesia...")
:mnesia.stop()

Logger.info("Deleting Schema...")
:mnesia.delete_schema([node()])

Logger.info("Mnesia Cleaned.")


------------------------------------------------------------
File: mix.exs
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule Acn.MixProject do
  use Mix.Project

  def project do
    [
      app: :acn,
      version: "0.1.0",
      elixir: "~> 1.14",
      start_permanent: Mix.env() == :prod,
      deps: deps()
    ]
  end

  # Run "mix help compile.app" to learn about applications.
  def application do
    [
      extra_applications: [:logger, :eex, :mnesia],
      mod: {ACN.Application, []}
    ]
  end

  # Run "mix help deps" to learn about dependencies.
  defp deps do
    [
      # JSON Parser: We handle data structures, not strings.
      {:jason, "~> 1.4"},

      # HTTP Client: We need to make API calls to external services.
      {:httpoison, "~> 2.0"},

      # JWT Token Library: We need to sign and verify security tokens.
      {:joken, "~> 2.6"},

      # Graph Data Structure: We need to build dependency graphs in memory.
      {:libgraph, "~> 0.16.0"},

      # YAML Parser: We need to read configuration files.
      {:yaml_elixir, "~> 2.9"},

      # Test Mocking: Only for the test environment.
      {:mimic, "~> 1.7", only: :test},

      # FIX: Pin jose to avoid OTP 25 compilation error
      {:jose, "1.11.10"},

      # Rustler for Firecracker NIFs
      {:rustler, "~> 0.29.1"},

      # AI / Embeddings / Tensors
      {:bumblebee, "~> 0.6.0"},
      {:exla, "~> 0.9.0"},
      {:nx, "~> 0.9.0"},

      # HTTP Client
      {:req, "~> 0.4.0"},

      # UUID
      {:elixir_uuid, "~> 1.2"},

      # Web / Phoenix (Compatible with Elixir 1.14)
      {:phoenix, "~> 1.6.0"},
      {:phoenix_html, "~> 3.0"},
      {:plug_cowboy, "~> 2.5"},

      # Hardware Interaction (Nerves)
      {:circuits_gpio, "~> 1.0"},

      # Observability: File-based logging
      {:logger_file_backend, "~> 0.0.13"}
    ]
  end
end

------------------------------------------------------------
File: genesis.exs
------------------------------------------------------------
# Genesis Launch Script
# This script initiates the first ACN Node and triggers the complete lifecycle

# 1. Ensure the application is running
{:ok, _} = Application.ensure_all_started(:acn)

# 2. Define the First Thought
genesis_payload = %{
  task: "Build a Hello World Elixir Module",
  task_type: :leaf, # Will trigger LEAF identity (not :manager)
  parent_node: nil    # Root Node
}

# 3. Wake the Supervisor (The Big Bang)
IO.puts("🚀 INITIATING GENESIS SEQUENCE...")
IO.puts("   Task: #{genesis_payload.task}")
IO.puts("   Type: #{genesis_payload.task_type}")
IO.puts("")

GenServer.cast(
  ACN.Core.NodeRegistry.lookup(:supervisor), 
  {:init_node, genesis_payload}
)

IO.puts("Genesis triggered. Observing autonomous lifecycle...")
IO.puts("(Press Ctrl+C twice to exit)")
IO.puts("")

# 4. Keep the script alive to see logs
Process.sleep(:infinity)


------------------------------------------------------------
File: build_artifacts.sh
------------------------------------------------------------
#!/bin/bash
set -e

# Configuration
KERNEL_URL="https://s3.amazonaws.com/spec.ccfc.min/img/quickstart_guide/x86_64/kernels/vmlinux-5.10.186"
ROOTFS_NAME="rootfs.ext4"
PRIV_DIR="priv"
NATIVE_DIR="native/acn_pilot"

echo "[Build] Starting ACN Firecracker Artifact Build..."

# 1. Ensure Priv Directory
mkdir -p $PRIV_DIR

# 2. Download Kernel (if missing)
if [ ! -f "$PRIV_DIR/vmlinux" ]; then
    echo "[Build] Downloading Kernel (vmlinux)..."
    curl -L -o "$PRIV_DIR/vmlinux" "$KERNEL_URL"
else
    echo "[Build] Kernel (vmlinux) exists. Skipping."
fi

# 3. Build ACN Pilot (Guest Agent)
echo "[Build] Compiling ACN Pilot (Rust)..."
if command -v cargo >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    cd $NATIVE_DIR
    # Build for musl (static) to run on Alpine
    rustup target add x86_64-unknown-linux-musl || true
    cargo build --release --target x86_64-unknown-linux-musl
    cp target/x86_64-unknown-linux-musl/release/acn_pilot ../../$PRIV_DIR/acn_pilot
    cd -
else
    echo "[Error] Cargo not found. Cannot build Guest Agent."
    exit 1
fi

# 4. Generate RootFS (via Docker)
echo "[Build] Generating RootFS via Docker..."
if command -v docker >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    # Create temp Dockerfile
    cat <<EOF > Dockerfile.rootfs
    FROM elixir:1.14-alpine
    # Install libs if needed, usually alpine is minimal
    # Copy Pilot as PID 1
    COPY priv/acn_pilot /sbin/init
    # Ensure proper permissions
    RUN chmod +x /sbin/init
    # Create mount points
    RUN mkdir -p /sys /proc /mnt/workspace
EOF

    # Build Image
    docker build -t acn_rootfs -f Dockerfile.rootfs .
    
    # Export to Tar
    id=$(docker create acn_rootfs)
    docker export $id > rootfs.tar
    docker rm $id
    
    # Convert Tar to Ext4 (requires virt-make-fs or root)
    if command -v virt-make-fs >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        echo "[Build] Converting tar to ext4..."
        virt-make-fs --format=raw --type=ext4 --size=+100M rootfs.tar $PRIV_DIR/$ROOTFS_NAME
        rm rootfs.tar
        rm Dockerfile.rootfs
        echo "[Success] RootFS created at $PRIV_DIR/$ROOTFS_NAME"
    else
        echo "[Warning] 'virt-make-fs' not found. Left 'rootfs.tar' for manual conversion."
        echo "Install libguestfs-tools or run: virt-make-fs --format=raw --type=ext4 rootfs.tar priv/rootfs.ext4"
    fi
else
    echo "[Error] Docker not found. Cannot build RootFS."
    exit 1
fi

echo "[Build] Complete."

// =============================================================================
// HSG PANOPTICON — Complete Dashboard v3
// Real family-tree hierarchy via mat_path | Premium node cards | Full controls
// =============================================================================

'use strict';
const { Socket } = Phoenix;

// ── Global State ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const nodeMap       = new Map();   // mat_path → NodeRecord
const eventLog      = [];          // chronological list of events
let totalEvents     = 0;
let channel         = null;
let startTime       = Date.now();
let selectedPath    = null;
let currentTransform = d3.zoomIdentity;

window.__getSelectedPath = () => selectedPath;

// ── Node Record Factory ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function makeNode(data) {
  const key = data.mat_path || data.node_id || data.id;
  return {
    id:           data.node_id || data.id || key,
    mat_path:     key,
    node_tier:    data.node_tier || 'leaf',
    phase:        data.phase_to || data.last_phase || 'pending',
    stress:       typeof data.stress === 'number' ? data.stress : 0,
    stressHistory:[],
    agents:       { cognitive: null, physical: null, security: null, autonomic: null },
    collapsed:    false,
    eventCount:   0,
    lastSeen:     Date.now(),
    traceId:      data.trace_id || null,
    type:         data.type || 'agent',
  };
}

// ── Colour System ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const PHASE_COLOR = {
  completed:    '#00ff9d',
  failed:       '#ff4757',
  frozen:       '#ff4757',
  running:      '#00a8ff',
  architecting: '#a55eea',
  execution:    '#00a8ff',
  initialized:  '#feca57',
  pending:      '#747d8c',
};

const TIER_BG = {
  root:         'rgba(26,10,46,0.92)',
  intermediate: 'rgba(10,20,46,0.92)',
  leaf:         'rgba(8,14,34,0.92)',
};

const TIER_BORDER = {
  root:         '#a55eea',
  intermediate: '#00e5ff',
  leaf:         '#747d8c',
};

const AGENT_COLOR = {
  cognitive:  '#00a8ff',
  physical:   '#00ff9d',
  security:   '#ff9f43',
  autonomic:  '#ff4757',
};

function phaseColor(phase) {
  return PHASE_COLOR[(phase || '').toLowerCase()] || '#747d8c';
}

function tierBorder(tier, phase) {
  const pc = phaseColor(phase);
  if (pc !== '#747d8c') return pc;
  return TIER_BORDER[tier] || '#747d8c';
}

function isRunning(p)   { return ['architecting','execution','running','initialized'].includes((p||'').toLowerCase()); }
function isCompleted(p) { return (p||'').toLowerCase() === 'completed'; }
function isErrored(p)   { const q = (p||'').toLowerCase(); return q === 'failed' || q === 'frozen'; }

// ── Node label ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function nodeLabel(mat_path) {
  const parts = (mat_path || '').split('.');
  let seg = parts[parts.length - 1] || mat_path;
  // If last segment is purely numeric, prefix parent scope
  if (/^\d+$/.test(seg) && parts.length > 1) {
    seg = parts[parts.length - 2] + ' #' + seg;
  }
  return seg.toUpperCase();
}

// ── Build Hierarchy from mat_path ─────────────────────────────────────────────
function buildHierarchy() {
  if (nodeMap.size === 0) return null;

  // Clone every known node into a fresh children-array structure
  const cloneMap = new Map();
  for (const [k, v] of nodeMap) {
    if (k) cloneMap.set(k, { ...v, children: [] });
  }

  // Auto-synthesize any missing ancestor nodes
  for (const key of Array.from(cloneMap.keys())) {
    const parts = key.split('.');
    for (let i = 1; i < parts.length; i++) {
      const parentPath = parts.slice(0, i).join('.');
      if (!cloneMap.has(parentPath)) {
        cloneMap.set(parentPath, {
          id: parentPath, mat_path: parentPath,
          node_tier: i === 1 ? 'intermediate' : 'leaf',
          phase: 'pending', stress: 0, agents: {},
          stressHistory: [], collapsed: false,
          eventCount: 0, lastSeen: 0, type: 'synthesized',
          children: [],
        });
      }
    }
  }

  // Wire parent → child
  const rootsList = [];
  cloneMap.forEach((v, k) => {
    const parts = k.split('.');
    if (parts.length === 1) {
      rootsList.push(v);
    } else {
      const parentPath = parts.slice(0, -1).join('.');
      const parent = cloneMap.get(parentPath);
      if (parent) parent.children.push(v);
      else rootsList.push(v);
    }
  });

  if (rootsList.length === 0) return null;

  // Apply collapse
  function applyCollapse(n) {
    const real = nodeMap.get(n.mat_path);
    if (real && real.collapsed && n.children.length > 0) {
      n._hiddenChildren = n.children;
      n.children = [];
    } else {
      n.children.sort((a, b) => a.mat_path.localeCompare(b.mat_path));
      n.children.forEach(applyCollapse);
    }
  }
  rootsList.forEach(applyCollapse);

  let root;
  if (rootsList.length === 1) {
    root = rootsList[0];
  } else {
    root = { mat_path: '__synth_root__', id: '__synth_root__', node_tier: 'root',
             phase: '', stress: 0, agents: {}, stressHistory: [], type: 'synthesized',
             children: rootsList };
  }
  return d3.hierarchy(root, d => d.children);
}

// ── SVG / D3 Setup ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const svg    = d3.select('#tree-svg');
const gMain  = svg.append('g').attr('class', 'g-main');
const gLinks = gMain.append('g').attr('class', 'g-links');
const gNodes = gMain.append('g').attr('class', 'g-nodes');

const miniSvg = d3.select('#minimap-svg');
const miniG   = miniSvg.append('g').attr('class', 'mini-root');

const NODE_W = 230;
const NODE_H = 100;
const H_GAP  = 60;
const V_GAP  = 80;

const treeLayout = d3.tree()
  .nodeSize([NODE_W + H_GAP, NODE_H + V_GAP])
  .separation((a, b) => a.parent === b.parent ? 1.1 : 1.5);

const zoom = d3.zoom()
  .scaleExtent([0.08, 4])
  .on('zoom', (e) => {
    currentTransform = e.transform;
    gMain.attr('transform', e.transform);
    updateMinimapViewport(e.transform);
  });

svg.call(zoom)
   .on('dblclick.zoom', null); // disable double-click zoom (we use it for node selection)

// ── Render ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
let renderScheduled = false;
function scheduleRender() {
  if (!renderScheduled) {
    renderScheduled = true;
    requestAnimationFrame(() => { renderScheduled = false; render(); });
  }
}

function render() {
  const hier = buildHierarchy();
  if (!hier) return;

  treeLayout(hier);

  const nodes = hier.descendants();
  const links = hier.links();

  // ── Links ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const linkSel = gLinks.selectAll('.tree-link')
    .data(links, d => d.target.data.mat_path);

  const linkEnter = linkSel.enter().append('path')
    .attr('class', 'tree-link')
    .attr('fill', 'none')
    .attr('stroke-width', 1.5)
    .attr('opacity', 0);

  linkEnter.merge(linkSel)
    .transition().duration(400)
    .attr('opacity', 1)
    .attr('stroke', d => {
      const phase = d.target.data.phase || '';
      return isRunning(phase) ? 'rgba(0,168,255,0.5)' :
             isCompleted(phase) ? 'rgba(0,255,157,0.35)' :
             isErrored(phase) ? 'rgba(255,71,87,0.4)' : 'rgba(100,120,200,0.2)';
    })
    .attr('d', d => {
      const sx = d.source.x, sy = d.source.y + NODE_H / 2;
      const tx = d.target.x, ty = d.target.y - NODE_H / 2;
      const my = (sy + ty) / 2;
      return `M${sx},${sy} C${sx},${my} ${tx},${my} ${tx},${ty}`;
    });

  linkSel.exit().transition().duration(200).attr('opacity', 0).remove();

  // ── Nodes ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const nodeSel = gNodes.selectAll('.tree-node')
    .data(nodes, d => d.data.mat_path);

  const nodeEnter = nodeSel.enter().append('foreignObject')
    .attr('class', 'tree-node')
    .attr('width', NODE_W)
    .attr('height', NODE_H)
    .attr('x', d => d.x - NODE_W / 2)
    .attr('y', d => d.y - NODE_H / 2)
    .attr('opacity', 0)
    .on('click', (evt, d) => { evt.stopPropagation(); selectNode(d.data); })
    .on('dblclick', (evt, d) => { evt.stopPropagation(); toggleCollapse(d.data.mat_path); });

  nodeEnter.append('xhtml:div').attr('class', 'node-card');

  const nodeMerge = nodeEnter.merge(nodeSel);

  nodeMerge.transition().duration(400)
    .attr('opacity', 1)
    .attr('x', d => d.x - NODE_W / 2)
    .attr('y', d => d.y - NODE_H / 2);

  nodeSel.exit().transition().duration(200).attr('opacity', 0).remove();

  // Update card content
  nodeMerge.each(function(d) {
    if (d.data.mat_path === '__synth_root__') return;
    renderCard(d3.select(this).select('.node-card'), d.data);
  });

  // Hide empty state
  if (nodes.length > 0) hideEmpty();

  // Update minimap
  renderMinimap(nodes, links);

  // Update topbar counters
  updateTopbar();
}

// ── Card Renderer ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const TIER_ICON = { root: '⬡', intermediate: '◈', leaf: '◆', synthesized: '◇' };

function renderCard(cardSel, nd) {
  const phase   = nd.phase || 'pending';
  const bc      = tierBorder(nd.node_tier, phase);
  const bgc     = TIER_BG[nd.node_tier] || 'rgba(8,14,34,0.92)';
  const running = isRunning(phase);
  const done    = isCompleted(phase);
  const errored = isErrored(phase);
  const selected = nd.mat_path === selectedPath;
  const synth    = nd.type === 'synthesized';

  // CSS class list
  let cls = 'node-card';
  if (running)  cls += ' nc-running';
  if (done)     cls += ' nc-done';
  if (errored)  cls += ' nc-error';
  if (selected) cls += ' nc-selected';
  if (synth)    cls += ' nc-synth';

  cardSel.node().className = cls;
  cardSel.node().style.cssText =
    `--bc:${bc};--bgc:${bgc};--pc:${phaseColor(phase)};`;

  // Heatmap bars
  const heat = (nd.stressHistory || []).slice(-8);
  const maxH = 18;
  const heatHtml = heat.map(s => {
    const v = Math.min(s || 0, 100) / 100;
    const h = Math.max(3, Math.round(v * maxH));
    const r = Math.round(v * 255);
    const g = Math.round((1 - v) * 200);
    return `<span class="hb" style="height:${h}px;background:rgb(${r},${g},60)"></span>`;
  }).join('');

  // Agent dots
  const agentHtml = ['cognitive','physical','security','autonomic'].map(k => {
    const ag = nd.agents ? nd.agents[k] : null;
    const color = AGENT_COLOR[k];
    const state = ag ? ag.state : 'none';
    return `<span class="ag-dot" data-state="${state}" style="--ac:${color}"
      title="${k}: ${state}" data-key="${k}" data-path="${escHtml(nd.mat_path)}"></span>`;
  }).join('');

  const label     = nodeLabel(nd.mat_path);
  const icon      = TIER_ICON[nd.node_tier] || '◆';
  const stress    = typeof nd.stress === 'number' ? nd.stress.toFixed(1) : '0.0';
  const evCount   = nd.eventCount || 0;
  const childCount = nd.children ? nd.children.length : 0;
  const phaseDisp = phase.toUpperCase();
  const tierDisp  = (nd.node_tier || 'node').toUpperCase();
  const lastSeen  = nd.lastSeen ? timeAgo(nd.lastSeen) : '—';

  cardSel.html(`
    <div class="nc-header">
      <span class="nc-icon">${icon}</span>
      <span class="nc-label" title="${escHtml(nd.mat_path)}">${escHtml(label)}</span>
      <span class="nc-tier">${tierDisp}</span>
    </div>
    <div class="nc-path">${escHtml(nd.mat_path)}</div>
    <div class="nc-mid">
      <div class="nc-agents">${agentHtml}</div>
      <div class="nc-heat">${heatHtml}</div>
    </div>
    <div class="nc-footer">
      <span class="nc-phase" style="color:var(--pc)">${phaseDisp}</span>
      <span class="nc-stat">⚡${stress}%</span>
      <span class="nc-stat">📨${evCount}</span>
      <span class="nc-stat">🕑${lastSeen}</span>
    </div>
  `);

  // Agent dot click
  cardSel.node().querySelectorAll('.ag-dot').forEach(el => {
    el.addEventListener('click', e => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      openAgentDetail(e.target.dataset.path, e.target.dataset.key);
    });
  });
}

// ── Selection / Detail Panel ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
function selectNode(nd) {
  if (nd.mat_path === '__synth_root__') return;
  selectedPath = nd.mat_path;
  renderDetailPanel(nd);
  scheduleRender();
  // Switch to detail tab
  switchSidebarTab('detail');
}

function renderDetailPanel(nd) {
  const el = document.getElementById('detail-body');
  if (!el) return;
  const bc = phaseColor(nd.phase);
  const childCount = nd.children ? nd.children.length : 0;
  const stress = typeof nd.stress === 'number' ? nd.stress.toFixed(1) : '0.0';
  const stressBar = `<div class="stress-track"><div class="stress-fill" style="width:${Math.min(nd.stress||0,100)}%;background:${bc}"></div></div>`;

  el.innerHTML = `
    <div class="dp-section">
      <div class="dp-label">IDENTITY</div>
      <div class="dp-row"><span class="dp-key">PATH</span><span class="dp-val mono">${escHtml(nd.mat_path)}</span></div>
      <div class="dp-row"><span class="dp-key">ID</span><span class="dp-val mono">${escHtml(nd.id||nd.mat_path)}</span></div>
      <div class="dp-row"><span class="dp-key">TIER</span><span class="dp-val">${escHtml(nd.node_tier||'—')}</span></div>
      <div class="dp-row"><span class="dp-key">TYPE</span><span class="dp-val">${escHtml(nd.type||'agent')}</span></div>
    </div>
    <div class="dp-section">
      <div class="dp-label">RUNTIME</div>
      <div class="dp-row"><span class="dp-key">PHASE</span><span class="dp-val" style="color:${bc}">${escHtml((nd.phase||'pending').toUpperCase())}</span></div>
      <div class="dp-row"><span class="dp-key">EVENTS</span><span class="dp-val">${nd.eventCount||0}</span></div>
      <div class="dp-row"><span class="dp-key">CHILDREN</span><span class="dp-val">${childCount}</span></div>
      <div class="dp-row"><span class="dp-key">LAST SEEN</span><span class="dp-val">${nd.lastSeen ? new Date(nd.lastSeen).toLocaleTimeString() : '—'}</span></div>
    </div>
    <div class="dp-section">
      <div class="dp-label">STRESS LEVEL — ${stress}%</div>
      ${stressBar}
    </div>
    <div class="dp-section">
      <div class="dp-label">AGENTS</div>
      ${['cognitive','physical','security','autonomic'].map(k => {
        const ag = nd.agents ? nd.agents[k] : null;
        const color = AGENT_COLOR[k];
        const state = ag ? ag.state : 'inactive';
        return `<div class="dp-row">
          <span class="dp-key" style="color:${color}">${k.toUpperCase()}</span>
          <span class="dp-val">${state}</span>
        </div>`;
      }).join('')}
    </div>
    <div class="dp-actions">
      <button class="dp-btn" onclick="toggleCollapse('${escHtml(nd.mat_path)}')">
        ⊞ TOGGLE SUBTREE
      </button>
      <button class="dp-btn dp-btn-accent" onclick="openShadowDrawer('${escHtml(nd.mat_path)}')">
        🗂 SHADOW FILES
      </button>
      <button class="dp-btn" onclick="fitToNode('${escHtml(nd.mat_path)}')">
        ⊙ FOCUS NODE
      </button>
    </div>
  `;
}

// ── Collapse ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function toggleCollapse(mat_path) {
  const nd = nodeMap.get(mat_path);
  if (nd) { nd.collapsed = !nd.collapsed; scheduleRender(); }
}
window.toggleCollapse = toggleCollapse;

// ── Fit to node ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
window.fitToNode = function(mat_path) {
  // Re-run layout to find x,y of node
  const hier = buildHierarchy();
  if (!hier) return;
  treeLayout(hier);
  const found = hier.descendants().find(d => d.data.mat_path === mat_path);
  if (!found) return;
  const svgEl = document.getElementById('tree-svg');
  const w = svgEl.clientWidth, h = svgEl.clientHeight;
  const scale = currentTransform.k;
  const tx = w / 2 - found.x * scale;
  const ty = h / 2 - found.y * scale;
  svg.transition().duration(600).call(
    zoom.transform, d3.zoomIdentity.translate(tx, ty).scale(scale)
  );
};

// ── Viewport Controls ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
window.vpZoom = function(factor) {
  svg.transition().duration(220).call(zoom.scaleBy, factor);
};

window.vpFitAll = function() {
  const hier = buildHierarchy();
  if (!hier) return;
  treeLayout(hier);
  const nodes = hier.descendants();
  if (nodes.length === 0) return;
  const xs = nodes.map(d => d.x), ys = nodes.map(d => d.y);
  const x0 = Math.min(...xs) - NODE_W, x1 = Math.max(...xs) + NODE_W;
  const y0 = Math.min(...ys) - NODE_H, y1 = Math.max(...ys) + NODE_H;
  const svgEl = document.getElementById('tree-svg');
  const w = svgEl.clientWidth, h = svgEl.clientHeight;
  const scale = Math.min(0.95 * w / (x1 - x0), 0.95 * h / (y1 - y0), 2);
  const tx = w / 2 - ((x0 + x1) / 2) * scale;
  const ty = h / 2 - ((y0 + y1) / 2) * scale;
  svg.transition().duration(600).call(
    zoom.transform, d3.zoomIdentity.translate(tx, ty).scale(scale)
  );
};

window.vpReset = function() {
  svg.transition().duration(500).call(
    zoom.transform, d3.zoomIdentity.translate(
      document.getElementById('tree-svg').clientWidth / 2, 80
    ).scale(0.9)
  );
};

// ── Minimap ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function renderMinimap(nodes, links) {
  const SCALE = 0.1;
  miniG.selectAll('.ml').data(links).join('line')
    .attr('class','ml')
    .attr('stroke','rgba(0,168,255,0.25)').attr('stroke-width',1)
    .attr('x1', d => d.source.x * SCALE).attr('y1', d => d.source.y * SCALE)
    .attr('x2', d => d.target.x * SCALE).attr('y2', d => d.target.y * SCALE);

  miniG.selectAll('.mn').data(nodes, d => d.data.mat_path).join('rect')
    .attr('class','mn')
    .attr('width', NODE_W * SCALE).attr('height', NODE_H * SCALE)
    .attr('x', d => (d.x - NODE_W/2) * SCALE).attr('y', d => (d.y - NODE_H/2) * SCALE)
    .attr('rx',1).attr('ry',1)
    .attr('fill', d => d.data.mat_path === selectedPath ? 'rgba(0,229,255,0.5)' : 'rgba(30,50,100,0.6)')
    .attr('stroke', d => phaseColor(d.data.phase)).attr('stroke-width', 0.8);
}

function updateMinimapViewport(t) {
  miniG.selectAll('.mv').remove();
  const svgEl = document.getElementById('tree-svg');
  if (!svgEl) return;
  const s = 0.1;
  const vpW = (svgEl.clientWidth / t.k) * s;
  const vpH = (svgEl.clientHeight / t.k) * s;
  const vpX = (-t.x / t.k) * s;
  const vpY = (-t.y / t.k) * s;
  miniG.append('rect').attr('class','mv')
    .attr('x', vpX).attr('y', vpY).attr('width', vpW).attr('height', vpH)
    .attr('fill','none').attr('stroke','rgba(0,229,255,0.7)').attr('stroke-width',1.5);
}

// ── State Mutations ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function upsertNode(data) {
  const key = data.mat_path || data.node_id || data.id;
  if (!key) return;

  // Guard: reject flat (depth-0) paths that are not already tracked as a root.
  // A single-segment key (no dots) that appears for the first time is almost
  // certainly an unresolved flat node_id whose ETS index lookup failed on the
  // backend. Inserting it would create a spurious second root → __synth_root__
  // wrapper → the original root node becomes a child of a blank rectangle.
  const isFlat = !key.includes('.');
  if (isFlat && !nodeMap.has(key)) {
    console.warn('[HSG] Dropped unresolved flat-path node:', key,
      '— expected a fully-qualified mat_path. Check ETS id_index seeding.');
    return;
  }

  if (!nodeMap.has(key)) {
    nodeMap.set(key, makeNode(data));
  }

  const nd = nodeMap.get(key);

  if (data.phase_to)    nd.phase = data.phase_to;
  if (data.last_phase)  nd.phase = data.last_phase;
  if (data.node_tier)   nd.node_tier = data.node_tier;

  if (typeof data.stress === 'number') {
    nd.stress = data.stress;
    nd.stressHistory.push(data.stress);
    if (nd.stressHistory.length > 20) nd.stressHistory.shift();
  }

  nd.lastSeen = Date.now();
  nd.eventCount = (nd.eventCount || 0) + 1;

  // Agent presence flags
  ['cognitive','physical','security','autonomic'].forEach(k => {
    const flag = data[`has_${k}`];
    if (flag === true) {
      nd.agents[k] = { state: isCompleted(nd.phase) ? 'done' : 'active' };
    } else if (flag === false && nd.agents[k]?.state === 'active') {
      nd.agents[k].state = 'done';
    }
  });

  if (isCompleted(nd.phase) || isErrored(nd.phase)) {
    Object.values(nd.agents).forEach(ag => { if (ag?.state === 'active') ag.state = 'done'; });
  }
}

function applySnapshot(data) {
  nodeMap.clear();
  (data.vertices || []).forEach(v => {
    upsertNode({
      node_id:    v.id,
      mat_path:   v.mat_path,
      node_tier:  v.node_tier,
      last_phase: v.last_phase,
      stress:     v.stress,
      type:       v.type,
    });
    // Override eventCount to 0 on snapshot (fresh load)
    const nd = nodeMap.get(v.mat_path);
    if (nd) nd.eventCount = 0;
  });
  scheduleRender();
}

function applyDiff(d) {
  upsertNode(d);
  totalEvents++;
  addEventLogEntry(d);
  addScrubberTick(d);
  scheduleRender();
}

// ── Topbar ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function updateTopbar() {
  const running   = [...nodeMap.values()].filter(n => isRunning(n.phase)).length;
  const completed = [...nodeMap.values()].filter(n => isCompleted(n.phase)).length;
  const errored   = [...nodeMap.values()].filter(n => isErrored(n.phase)).length;

  setText('m-nodes',     nodeMap.size);
  setText('m-running',   running);
  setText('m-completed', completed);
  setText('m-errors',    errored);
  setText('m-events',    totalEvents);

  const secs = Math.floor((Date.now() - startTime) / 1000);
  const h = String(Math.floor(secs / 3600)).padStart(2,'0');
  const m = String(Math.floor((secs % 3600) / 60)).padStart(2,'0');
  const s = String(secs % 60).padStart(2,'0');
  setText('m-uptime', `${h}:${m}:${s}`);
}

setInterval(updateTopbar, 1000);

function setText(id, v) {
  const el = document.getElementById(id);
  if (el) el.textContent = v;
}

// ── Sidebar Tabs ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
window.switchSidebarTab = function(tab) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.stab-btn').forEach(b => {
    b.classList.toggle('stab-active', b.dataset.tab === tab);
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.stab-panel').forEach(p => {
    p.style.display = p.id === `stab-${tab}` ? 'flex' : 'none';
  });
};

// ── Event Log ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const MAX_LOG = 60;

function addEventLogEntry(d) {
  const entry = {
    ts:         Date.now(),
    node_id:    d.node_id || d.mat_path,
    mat_path:   d.mat_path,
    phase_from: d.phase_from || '',
    phase_to:   d.phase_to || '',
    stress:     d.stress,
  };
  eventLog.unshift(entry);
  if (eventLog.length > MAX_LOG) eventLog.pop();
  renderEventLog();
}

function renderEventLog() {
  const container = document.getElementById('event-log-list');
  if (!container) return;
  container.innerHTML = eventLog.slice(0, 30).map(e => {
    const ts   = new Date(e.ts).toLocaleTimeString();
    const pc   = phaseColor(e.phase_to);
    const label = nodeLabel(e.mat_path || e.node_id || '');
    return `
      <div class="log-row">
        <span class="log-ts">${ts}</span>
        <span class="log-node" title="${escHtml(e.mat_path||'')}">${escHtml(label)}</span>
        <span class="log-phase" style="color:${pc};border-color:${pc}40">
          ${escHtml(e.phase_from||'?')}→${escHtml(e.phase_to||'?')}
        </span>
      </div>`;
  }).join('');
}

// ── Scrubber ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function addScrubberTick(d) {
  const track = document.getElementById('scrubber-track');
  if (!track) return;
  const tick = document.createElement('div');
  tick.className = 's-tick';
  tick.style.background = phaseColor(d.phase_to);
  tick.title = `${d.node_id}: ${d.phase_from}→${d.phase_to}`;
  track.appendChild(tick);
  while (track.children.length > 250) track.removeChild(track.firstChild);
  track.scrollLeft = track.scrollWidth;
}

// ── Shadow Drawer ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function openShadowDrawer(mat_path) {
  const drawer = document.getElementById('shadow-drawer');
  const title  = document.getElementById('shadow-title');
  const body   = document.getElementById('shadow-body');

  title.textContent = `⬡ ${mat_path}`;
  body.innerHTML = '<div class="loading">Loading shadow files…</div>';
  drawer.classList.add('open');

  try {
    const res  = await fetch(`/api/shadow/${encodeURIComponent(mat_path)}`);
    const data = await res.json();
    renderShadowTabs(data, body);
  } catch(e) {
    body.innerHTML = `<div class="err">Error: ${e.message}</div>`;
  }
}
window.openShadowDrawer = openShadowDrawer;

function renderShadowTabs(data, container) {
  const tabs = ['cognitive','physical','security','autonomic'];
  const tabBar = tabs.map(t =>
    `<button class="stab" data-stab="${t}" onclick="switchShadowStab('${t}')">${t.toUpperCase()}</button>`
  ).join('');

  const panels = tabs.map(t => {
    const content = data[t]
      ? `<pre class="shadow-pre">${escHtml(data[t])}</pre>`
      : '<div class="empty-msg">No data recorded yet.</div>';
    return `<div class="stab-content" id="sstab-${t}" style="display:none">${content}</div>`;
  }).join('');

  container.innerHTML = `<div class="stab-bar tabbar">${tabBar}</div>${panels}`;
  switchShadowStab('cognitive');
}

window.switchShadowStab = function(tab) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.stab[data-stab]').forEach(b =>
    b.classList.toggle('stab-active', b.dataset.stab === tab)
  );
  document.querySelectorAll('.stab-content').forEach(p =>
    p.style.display = p.id === `sstab-${tab}` ? 'block' : 'none'
  );
};

window.closeShadowDrawer = function() {
  document.getElementById('shadow-drawer').classList.remove('open');
};

// ── Agent Detail (via shadow drawer) ─────────────────────────────────────────
async function openAgentDetail(mat_path, agent_key) {
  const drawer = document.getElementById('shadow-drawer');
  const title  = document.getElementById('shadow-title');
  const body   = document.getElementById('shadow-body');

  title.textContent = `${agent_key.toUpperCase()} @ ${mat_path}`;
  body.innerHTML = '<div class="loading">Fetching agent record…</div>';
  drawer.classList.add('open');

  try {
    const res  = await fetch(`/api/shadow/${encodeURIComponent(mat_path)}`);
    const data = await res.json();
    if (agent_key === 'cognitive' && data.cognitive) {
      const lines = data.cognitive.trim().split('\n').filter(Boolean).slice(-5);
      const parsed = lines.map(l => { try { return JSON.parse(l); } catch { return null; } }).filter(Boolean);
      const entry = parsed[parsed.length - 1];
      if (!entry) { body.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-msg">No cognitive entries.</div>'; return; }
      body.innerHTML = `
        <div class="agent-panel">
          <div class="ap-row"><div class="ap-key">MODEL</div><div class="ap-val">${escHtml(entry.model||'—')}</div></div>
          <div class="ap-row"><div class="ap-key">TOKENS ↑↓</div><div class="ap-val">${entry.token_count_in||0} / ${entry.token_count_out||0}</div></div>
          <div class="ap-row"><div class="ap-key">ACCEPTED</div><div class="ap-val" style="color:${entry.result_accepted?'#00ff9d':'#ff4757'}">${entry.result_accepted}</div></div>
          <div class="ap-row"><div class="ap-key">PROMPT</div></div>
          <pre class="shadow-pre">${escHtml(entry.prompt||'(not recorded)')}</pre>
          <div class="ap-row"><div class="ap-key">RESPONSE</div></div>
          <pre class="shadow-pre">${escHtml(entry.response||'(not recorded)')}</pre>
        </div>`;
    } else {
      body.innerHTML = data[agent_key]
        ? `<pre class="shadow-pre">${escHtml(data[agent_key])}</pre>`
        : '<div class="empty-msg">No data for this agent.</div>';
    }
  } catch(e) {
    body.innerHTML = `<div class="err">Error: ${e.message}</div>`;
  }
}
window.openAgentDetail = openAgentDetail;

// ── Query Modal ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
window.runQuery = function(type, extra = {}) {
  if (!channel) return;
  channel.push('query', { type, ...extra }).receive('ok', () => {});
};

window.requestSnapshot = function() {
  if (!channel) return;
  channel.push('get_snapshot', {}).receive('ok', data => applySnapshot(data));
};

function showQueryModal(data) {
  const TITLES = {
    rejected_prompts: '🧠 Rejected Prompts',
    unauthorized_ops: '🔐 Unauthorized Ops',
    stress_spikes:    '⚡ Stress Spikes',
    replay_physical:  '🗂 Physical Replay',
  };
  document.getElementById('modal-title').textContent = TITLES[data.type] || data.type;
  const body = document.getElementById('modal-body');
  const rows = data.rows || [];
  if (!rows.length) {
    body.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-msg">No results found.</div>';
  } else {
    const hdr = rows[0].map((_, i) => `<th>COL ${i+1}</th>`).join('');
    const bdy = rows.map(r => '<tr>' + r.map(c => `<td>${escHtml(c)}</td>`).join('') + '</tr>').join('');
    body.innerHTML = `<table class="q-table"><thead><tr>${hdr}</tr></thead><tbody>${bdy}</tbody></table>`;
  }
  document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.add('open');
}
window.closeModal = () => document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.remove('open');

// ── Utils ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function escHtml(s) {
  return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
}

function timeAgo(ts) {
  const d = Math.floor((Date.now() - ts) / 1000);
  if (d < 5)   return 'now';
  if (d < 60)  return `${d}s`;
  if (d < 3600) return `${Math.floor(d/60)}m`;
  return `${Math.floor(d/3600)}h`;
}

function hideEmpty() {
  const el = document.getElementById('empty-state');
  if (el) el.style.display = 'none';
}

// ── Phoenix WebSocket ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function connect() {
  const socket = new Socket('/dashboard', {});
  socket.connect();
  channel = socket.channel('acn:panopticon', {});

  channel.on('snapshot',    data => { nodeMap.clear(); totalEvents = 0; applySnapshot(data); });
  channel.on('graph_diff',  data => applyDiff(data));
  channel.on('query_result', data => showQueryModal(data));

  channel.join()
    .receive('ok',    ()  => console.log('[HSG] Channel joined'))
    .receive('error', err => console.error('[HSG] Channel error', err));
}

// ── Bootstrap ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
connect();
updateTopbar();
// Initial viewport
setTimeout(() => {
  svg.call(zoom.transform, d3.zoomIdentity.translate(
    (document.getElementById('tree-svg')?.clientWidth || 800) / 2, 80
  ).scale(0.9));
}, 100);

name: "tm_planner"
description: "Generates a batched execution plan (DAG) for a task."
model: "gemini-3-flash-preview"
temperature: 0.5

system_prompt: |
  You are the **Task Planner** of the ACN.
  Your goal is to break down a HIGH-LEVEL TASK into a series of **Batched Sub-Tasks** (DAG).
  Do not include creation of folders or file structure or environment preparation. Just focus on the tasks involved in writing the code, tests and documentation.
  
  **LANGUAGE AGNOSTICISM**:
  - Respect the conventions of the target language requested in the description (e.g. use `src/` for Java, `module/` for Python if appropriate).
  - do not put the two tasks in the same file is one need the other. in this case the higher one in hierarchy must be performed first

  IMPORTANT: THE PLAN MUST SATISFY THESE FIVE AREAS OF A STANDARD PRODUCT:
    1. IT MUST BE FEATURE RICH
    2. IT MUST BE FUNCTIONAL
    4. APART FROM INFASTRUCTURE, IT MUST ALSO COVER THE ACTUAL LOGIC TO ACHIEVE THE GOAL OF THE SYSTEM
    5. THE PRODUCT MUST BE BUILT TO HANDLE MASSIVE SCALING AND CONCURRENCY 
    6. SECURITY MUST BE A TOP PRIORITY
    7. IT MUST BE TOTALLY AND RADICALLY COMPLETE 
    8. THE FIRST STEP MUST BE A REQUIREMENTS FILE FOR DEPENDENCY MANAGEMENT
    9. DO NOT INCLUDE TESTS OR DOCUMENTATION IN THE PLAN. FOCUS ON THE LOGIC AND CODE IMPLEMENTATION


  APART FROM INFASTRUCTURE, IT MUST ALSO COVER THE ACTUAL LOGIC, THE BRAIN TO ACHIEVE THE GOAL OF THE SYSTEM
  APART FROM INFASTRUCTURE, IT MUST ALSO COVER THE ACTUAL LOGIC, THE BRAIN TO ACHIEVE THE GOAL OF THE SYSTEM
  APART FROM INFASTRUCTURE, IT MUST ALSO COVER THE ACTUAL LOGIC, THE BRAIN TO ACHIEVE THE GOAL OF THE SYSTEM
  DO NOT INCLUDE TESTS OR DOCUMENTATION IN THE PLAN. FOCUS ON THE LOGIC AND CODE IMPLEMENTATION
  - **TARGET PATH**: <%= @deep_context.path %>

  🧠 RECURSION & GRANULARITY CONTROL (CRITICAL)
  You are a node in a Recursive Planning System. Your output is fed into another copy of this prompt. You must decide if you are designing Architecture or Implementation.

  HOW TO DETERMINE GRANULARITY: Analyze your TARGET PATH and the TASK description.

  SCENARIO A: The "Architect" (High Complexity)
  Condition: The Task involves a full feature set (e.g., "Build Auth System", "Create Payment Module") or the Path is a root/module directory.

  Action: Break the job into Logical Sub-Systems.

  Output: Define tasks that represent entire folders or major components.
  Example: Instead of listing "Create LoginView.swift", "Create LoginController.swift", "Create User.swift", you simply output ONE task: {"id": "auth_flow", "description": "Implement the Authentication Logic and Views", "path": ".../auth"}.
  Why? The downstream agent will pick up .../auth and break it down into files.

  SCENARIO B: The "Developer" (Low Complexity)

  Condition: The Task is specific (e.g., "Implement email regex validator", "Create SQL migration for Users").
  Action: Break the job into Atomic Files.
  Output: Define tasks that map 1:1 to specific files.

  STOPPING HEURISTIC (When to stop decomposing):
  If a sub-task requires multiple files to function (e.g., a View and a Logic Controller), DO NOT SPLIT THEM YET. Keep them grouped as one task. The next agent will split them.

  If you describe a task using words like "System", "Module", or "Engine", it is NOT atomic.

  If you describe a task using words like "Function", "Class", "Query", or "Interface", it IS atomic.

  ### 🗣️ SEMANTIC VOCABULARY PROTOCOL (NOUN BINDING)
  The specific **NOUNS** you use to describe the object of the task dictate the granularity. You MUST use the correct terminology to signal depth to the downstream planner.

  **SCENARIO A: HIGH-LEVEL (Directory/Container)**
  * **MANDATORY NOUNS**: Use words that imply a collection of files or a broad responsibility.
      * *Keywords*: "Module", "Subsystem", "Service", "Engine", "Layer", "Cluster", "Package", "Interface Suite".
  * **DESCRIPTION STYLE**: "Setup the Authentication **Module**..." or "Design the Payments **Subsystem**..."
  * *Signal*: When the next agent sees "Module", it knows it must break this down into files (Controllers, Views, etc.).

  **SCENARIO B: ATOMIC-LEVEL (File/Code)**
  * **MANDATORY NOUNS**: Use words that imply a single file or specific code entity.
      * *Keywords*: "Class", "Script", "Function", "Query", "Schema", "Endpoint", "hook", "Config", "View".
  * **DESCRIPTION STYLE**: "Implement the Login **Controller Class** in login_controller.py..." 
  * *Signal*: When the next agent sees "Class", it knows it is a leaf node and will generate code.

  Here are 3 specific "Wrong vs. Correct" examples covering different backend paradigms.
  Example 1: The "Hallucinated Import" Trap (Python / FastAPI)
  Task: Build a user API that saves to a database.
  ❌ The WRONG Way (Parallel Execution)
  Attempting to build the API endpoint and the Database Model at the same time.
  ```json
  {
    "batches": [
      [
        { "id": "models", "description": "Create User SQLAlchemy model" },
        { "id": "api", "description": "Create FastAPI route that imports User and saves data" }
      ]
    ]
  }
  ```
  💀 Why this fails:
  The AI writing api creates code like user = User(name="John", email="...").
  The AI writing models creates code like class User(Base): name = Column(String).
  The Bug: The api might assume there is an id field or a set_password() method that the models file simply didn't create. When you run the code: AttributeError: 'User' object has no attribute 'set_password'.
  ✅ The CORRECT Way (Sequential Context)
  Define the shape of the data first. Then build the logic that uses it.
  ```json
  {
    "batches": [
      [ { "id": "db_setup", "description": "Create database.py with Base and Session", "path": "/models" } ],
      [ { "id": "models", "description": "Create User model inheriting from Base", "path": "/models" } ],
      [ { "id": "api", "description": "Create FastAPI route using User model", "path": "/api" } ]
    ]
  }
  ```

  Rating: 4/10 (Conceptually Sound, Practically Flawed)
  🔴 Critical Flaws
  1. The "Data Vacuum" (Missing Schemas)
  You defined "Specs" (Behaviors) in Batch 2, but you completely forgot Data Definitions (Structs & Schemas).
  The Problem: search_coordinator_impl (Batch 3) will try to query the database. But the Ecto Schemas (the definition of the database tables) are missing from the plan!
  The Result: The AI will write query code like from(p in Project, where: ...) but the Project module/struct does not exist. The code will fail to compile.
  2. The Ecto Dependency Loop
  You have persistent_store_impl (The Database Repo) in the same batch as search_coordinator_impl (The Consumer of the Repo).
  The Problem: The Coordinator needs to inject the Repo. If the Repo module hasn't been generated yet, the Coordinator might guess the wrong functions (e.g., Repo.insert vs Repo.create).
  The Fix: The Database layer (Repo + Schemas) must exist before the business logic that queries it.
  3. "Specs" vs. "Behaviours" Overkill
  The Problem: Creating a separate _spec file for every single GenServer (port_manager_spec, tui_spec) is not idiomatic Elixir.
  Why: In Elixir, the "Public API" of a module is usually its spec. Unless you are planning to have multiple interchangeable implementations (e.g., MockPortManager vs RealPortManager), defining a separate Behavior file for every actor is waste. It confuses the AI and uses up context tokens on boilerplate.
  4. The Missing "Agent"
  The Problem: You have a SessionManager which manages agents, but you forgot to include the Agent code in the plan! The SessionManager needs to spawn a ProCoder.Agent process. If that module doesn't exist, the manager crashes.
  🟢 The Corrected Elixir DAG
  In Elixir/OTP, the hierarchy is Data -> Workers -> Managers -> Supervisors.
  Batch 1: Configuration
  mix.exs (Dependencies)
  config (Config files)
  Batch 2: The Data Layer (CRITICAL STEP)
  ecto_repo (The Repo module)
  schemas (The Ecto Schemas: Project, Session, Memory).
  structs (Other pure data structures).
  Why? All subsequent logic depends on the shape of this data.
  Batch 3: Independent Workers (The Tools)
  connection_pool (Depends only on deps)
  port_manager (Depends only on System)
  context_store (ETS wrapper)
  tui_view (Pure view logic, not the controller)
  Batch 4: Business Logic (The Consumers)
  search_coordinator (Uses Repo & Schemas from Batch 2)
  agent_core (The logic for the bot, uses Tools from Batch 3)
  session_manager (Manages the Agents)
  Batch 5: Orchestration
  main_supervisor (Starts Repo, SessionManager, etc.)
  application (Entry point)
  }

File: telemetry.ex

defmodule ACNWeb.Telemetry do
  @moduledoc """
  Telemetry supervisor for ACN metrics.
  """
  use Supervisor

  def start_link(arg) do
    Supervisor.start_link(__MODULE__, arg, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  @impl true
  def init(_arg) do
    # No children for now - metrics will be added later
    Supervisor.init([], strategy: :one_for_one)
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: endpoint.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule ACNWeb.Endpoint do
  use Phoenix.Endpoint, otp_app: :acn

  socket "/dashboard", ACNWeb.DashboardSocket,
    websocket: [check_origin: false]

  plug Plug.Static,
    at: "/",
    from: :acn,
    gzip: false,
    only: ~w(assets fonts images js favicon.ico robots.txt)

  # Code reloading can be explicitly enabled under the
  # :code_reloader configuration of your endpoint.
  if code_reloading? do
    plug Phoenix.CodeReloader
  end

  plug Plug.RequestId
  plug Plug.Telemetry, event_prefix: [:phoenix, :endpoint]

  plug Plug.Parsers,
    parsers: [:urlencoded, :multipart, :json],
    pass: ["*/*"],
    json_decoder: Phoenix.json_library()

  plug Plug.MethodOverride
  plug Plug.Head
  plug Plug.Session, store: :cookie, key: "_acn_key", signing_salt: "random_salt"
  plug ACNWeb.Router
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: router.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule ACNWeb.Router do
  use Phoenix.Router

  pipeline :browser do
    plug :accepts, ["html"]
    plug :fetch_session
    plug :protect_from_forgery
    plug :put_secure_browser_headers
  end

  pipeline :api do
    plug :accepts, ["json"]
  end

  scope "/" do
    pipe_through :browser
    get "/", ACNWeb.PageController, :index
  end

  scope "/api" do
    pipe_through :api
    get "/shadow/:node_id", ACNWeb.PageController, :shadow
  end
end

defmodule ACNWeb.DashboardSocket do
  use Phoenix.Socket

  channel "acn:node:*", ACNWeb.NodeChannel
  channel "acn:panopticon", ACNWeb.PanopticonChannel

  @impl true
  def connect(_params, socket, _connect_info) do
    # Concrete Implementation: Assign Anonymous ID
    socket = assign(socket, :user_id, UUID.uuid4())
    {:ok, socket}
  end

  @impl true
  def id(socket), do: "user_socket:#{socket.assigns.user_id}"
end


------------------------------------------------------------

defmodule ACNWeb.NodeChannel do
  use Phoenix.Channel
  require Logger

  @impl true
  def join("acn:node:" <> _node_id, _payload, socket) do
    {:ok, socket}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_in("command", %{"text" => user_input}, socket) do
    # Use AI to triage the command
    vars = %{user_input: user_input}

    case ACN.Core.ProfileLoader.load("messenger_dispatcher", vars) do
      {:ok, profile} ->
        buffer = ACN.AI.ContextBuffer.new(profile["system_prompt"])
        adapter = Application.get_env(:acn, :ai)[:default_adapter] || ACN.AI.Adapters.OllamaAdapter
        config = %{adapter: adapter, opts: [model: profile["model"], temperature: profile["temperature"]]}

        triage_result =
          case ACN.AI.Pipeline.run(buffer, [], config) do
            {:ok, msg, _} ->
              try do
                Jason.decode!(msg.content)
              rescue
                _ -> %{"type" => "garbage"}
              end
            _ -> %{"type" => "error"}
          end

        Logger.info("Command Triage: #{inspect(triage_result)}")

        # Route based on triage
        case triage_result["type"] do
          "command" ->
            route_command(triage_result, socket)
            {:reply, {:ok, %{status: "dispatched", triage: triage_result}}, socket}

          "query" ->
            handle_query(triage_result, socket)
            {:reply, {:ok, %{status: "answered"}}, socket}

          _ ->
            {:reply, {:error, %{reason: "Invalid command"}}, socket}
        end

      {:error, reason} ->
        Logger.error("Failed to load Dispatcher profile: #{inspect(reason)}")
        {:reply, {:error, %{reason: "Internal error"}}, socket}
    end
  end

  defp route_command(%{"target" => target, "intent" => intent}, _socket) when not is_nil(target) do
    # Resolve target agent via Registry
    target_atom = String.to_atom(target)
    pid = ACN.Core.NodeRegistry.whereis_name({target_atom, nil})

    case pid do
      pid when is_pid(pid) ->
        # Send message based on intent
        message = build_message(intent)
        GenServer.cast(pid, message)
        Logger.info("Routed command '#{intent}' to #{target}")

      :undefined ->
        Logger.warning("Target agent '#{target}' not found")
    end
  end

  defp route_command(_, _), do: :ok

  defp handle_query(%{"intent" => "get_status"}, socket) do
    # Broadcast a status request
    Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(ACN.PubSub, "internal:raw_events", {:event, {:status_request, self()}})
    push(socket, "status_update", %{message: "Status requested"})
  end

  defp handle_query(_, _), do: :ok

  defp build_message("pause_governance"), do: {:pause_governance}
  defp build_message("resume"), do: {:resume_governance}
  defp build_message("inject_pain"), do: {:pain_signal, 50}
  defp build_message(_), do: {:unknown_command}
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: panopticon_channel.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule ACNWeb.PanopticonChannel do
  @moduledoc """
  HSG Dashboard — Real-time Panopticon Bridge.

  Bridges the PubSub telemetry stream into browser WebSocket connections.
  Any browser that joins "acn:panopticon" receives:
    - An immediate full graph snapshot on join
    - Incremental graph diffs on every MicroDBC telemetry event
    - Query results on demand
  """
  use Phoenix.Channel
  require Logger
  alias ACN.Observability.{Panopticon, Query, MicroDBC}

  @impl true
  def join("acn:panopticon", _payload, socket) do
    Phoenix.PubSub.subscribe(ACN.PubSub, MicroDBC.global_topic())
    send(self(), :push_snapshot)
    {:ok, socket}
  end

  # --- Inbound Browser Events ---

  @impl true
  def handle_in("get_snapshot", _payload, socket) do
    {:reply, {:ok, Panopticon.snapshot()}, socket}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_in("query", %{"type" => "rejected_prompts"}, socket) do
    rows = Query.rejected_prompts()
    push(socket, "query_result", %{type: "rejected_prompts", rows: format_rows(rows)})
    {:noreply, socket}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_in("query", %{"type" => "unauthorized_ops", "node_id" => node_id}, socket) do
    rows = Query.unauthorized_ops(node_id)
    push(socket, "query_result", %{type: "unauthorized_ops", node_id: node_id, rows: format_rows(rows)})
    {:noreply, socket}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_in("query", %{"type" => "stress_spikes"}, socket) do
    rows = Query.stress_spike_prompts(80.0)
    push(socket, "query_result", %{type: "stress_spikes", rows: format_rows(rows)})
    {:noreply, socket}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_in("query", %{"type" => "replay_physical", "node_id" => node_id}, socket) do
    rows = Query.replay_physical_state(node_id)
    push(socket, "query_result", %{type: "replay_physical", node_id: node_id, rows: format_rows(rows)})
    {:noreply, socket}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_in(_event, _payload, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}

  # --- Server-side info ---

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:push_snapshot, socket) do
    push(socket, "snapshot", Panopticon.snapshot())
    {:noreply, socket}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info({:telemetry_event, %MicroDBC{} = dbc}, socket) do
    raw_path = dbc.mat_path || dbc.node_id || ""

    # Only push diff if this is a real agent node (valid dot-notation path)
    if valid_mat_path?(raw_path) do
      # Resolve flat paths (e.g. "dependency_management") to canonical mat_paths
      # ("root.dependency_management") via the Panopticon's intent index
      mat_path =
        case :ets.lookup(:acn_hsg_id_index, raw_path) do
          [{^raw_path, full}] -> full
          [] -> raw_path
        end

      parts  = String.split(mat_path, ".")
      depth  = length(parts) - 1

      # Guard: if the resolved path is still flat (depth=0) AND it is NOT a known
      # node in the ETS node table, it means the index lookup failed to expand it.
      # Broadcasting a flat orphan ID as a "root" node would create a spurious second
      # root in the dashboard, triggering the __synth_root__ wrapper bug.
      # Skip these until the node is properly seeded via inject_intent / register_node.
      is_known_node = depth > 0 or match?([{^mat_path, _}], :ets.lookup(:acn_hsg_nodes, mat_path))

      if is_known_node do
        parent_id = if depth > 0, do: Enum.take(parts, depth) |> Enum.join("."), else: nil

        tier =
          cond do
            depth == 0 -> "root"
            depth == 1 -> "intermediate"
            true       -> "leaf"
          end

        diff = %{
          type:          "event",
          node_id:       mat_path,
          mat_path:      mat_path,
          parent_id:     parent_id,
          depth:         depth,
          node_tier:     tier,
          phase_from:    to_string(dbc.phase_from),
          phase_to:      to_string(dbc.phase_to),
          timestamp:     DateTime.to_iso8601(dbc.timestamp),
          stress:        get_in(dbc.autonomic, [:stress_level]) || 0.0,
          has_cognitive: not is_nil(dbc.cognitive),
          has_physical:  not is_nil(dbc.physical),
          has_security:  not is_nil(dbc.security),
          has_autonomic: not is_nil(dbc.autonomic)
        }

        push(socket, "graph_diff", diff)
      else
        Logger.debug("PanopticonChannel: Dropped orphan flat-path diff '#{mat_path}' (not yet registered in node table)")
      end
    end

    {:noreply, socket}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(_msg, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}

  # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  # Helpers
  # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

  # Mirrors the validation in Panopticon — ensures we never push phantom nodes
  defp valid_mat_path?(nil), do: false
  defp valid_mat_path?(""), do: false
  defp valid_mat_path?(s) when is_binary(s) do
    String.split(s, ".")
    |> Enum.all?(fn seg ->
      byte_size(seg) > 0 and
      byte_size(seg) <= 80 and
      String.match?(seg, ~r/^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_]*$/)
    end)
  end
  defp valid_mat_path?(_), do: false

  defp format_rows(rows) when is_list(rows) do
    Enum.map(rows, fn row when is_tuple(row) ->
      row |> Tuple.to_list() |> Enum.map(&inspect/1)
    end)
  end
end

defmodule ACNWeb.PageController do
  use Phoenix.Controller, namespace: ACNWeb

  # ── Main Dashboard ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  def index(conn, _params) do
    html(conn, dashboard_html())
  end

  # ── Shadow File API ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  def shadow(conn, %{"node_id" => node_id}) do
    # node_id is actually the mat_path, e.g. "root.auth.db"
    workspace_root =
      Application.get_env(:acn, :hsg)[:shadow_root] || "tmp/acn_workspaces"
    workspace_root = Path.expand(workspace_root)

    # Derive shadow dir: root.auth.db → <workspace_root>/root/.shadow/auth/db
    parts = String.split(node_id, ".")
    [root_node | rest_path] = parts
    shadow_dir = Path.join([workspace_root, root_node, ".shadow"] ++ rest_path)

    result = %{
      cognitive: read_shadow_file(shadow_dir, "cognitive.jsonl"),
      physical:  read_shadow_file(shadow_dir, "physical.patch"),
      security:  read_shadow_file(shadow_dir, "security.audit"),
      autonomic: read_shadow_file(shadow_dir, "autonomic.log")
    }

    json(conn, result)
  end

  defp read_shadow_file(dir, filename) do
    path = Path.join(dir, filename)
    case File.read(path) do
      {:ok, content} -> content
      {:error, _}    -> nil
    end
  end

  # ── Dashboard HTML ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  defp dashboard_html do
    ~S"""

------------------------------------------------------------
File: application.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule ACN.Application do
  @moduledoc false
  use Application

  alias ACN.Core.NodeRegistry

  @impl true
  def start(_type, _args) do
    # Define optional children based on config
    llm_children = 
      if Application.get_env(:acn, :enable_local_serving, false) do
        [ACN.AI.LLM.Serving] 
      else
        []
      end

    children = [
      # Infrastructure (Must start first)
      {Finch, name: ACN.HTTPClient, pools: %{:default => [size: 200, count: 1]}},
      ACN.Infrastructure.ClusterManager,
      ACN.Infrastructure.StateBackup,
      ACN.Infrastructure.NetworkMonitor,
      ACN.Infrastructure.ACMSLimiter,
      ACN.Core.AuditBuffer,

      # Core Registry & Monitors (Before agents)
      NodeRegistry.child_spec(),
      ACN.Core.LatticeRegistry,
      ACN.Core.NodeMonitor,
      ACN.Core.NodeSupervisor,

      # Phoenix PubSub (BEFORE agents that subscribe to it)
      {Phoenix.PubSub, name: ACN.PubSub},

      # HSG Observability Layer
      ACN.Observability.Schema,
      ACN.Observability.Panopticon,

      # Root Supervisor (Responsible for starting the root enclave)
      # REMOVED: Genesis now dynamically spawns the root node via NodeSupervisor.
      # {ACN.Agents.SupervisorAgent, [node_id: "root"]},

      # AI Services
      {Task.Supervisor, name: ACN.AI.TaskSupervisor},

      # Phoenix Web (After agents)
      ACNWeb.Endpoint
    ] ++ llm_children ++ (if Application.get_env(:acn, :enable_embeddings, false), do: [ACN.AI.Embedder.Serving], else: [])

    opts = [strategy: :one_for_one, name: ACN.Supervisor, max_restarts: 10, max_seconds: 60]
    Supervisor.start_link(children, opts)
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: ai.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule ACN.AI do
  @moduledoc """
  Public API for the Artificial Intelligence subsystems of the ACN.
  Facades the Cognitive Pipeline and Embedding Engine.
  """

  alias ACN.AI.Pipeline
  alias ACN.AI.ContextBuffer
  alias ACN.AI.Embedder

  @doc """
  Runs a chat session with the AI.
  Wraps Pipeline.run/3.
  """
  def chat(messages, tools \\ [], opts \\ []) do
     # Convert raw list of maps to ContextBuffer if needed, or create new
     buffer =
       if is_list(messages) do
         ContextBuffer.new(messages)
       else
         messages
       end

     adapter = Application.get_env(:acn, :ai)[:default_adapter] || ACN.AI.Adapters.OllamaAdapter
     
     config = %{
       adapter: adapter,
       opts: opts
     }

     case Pipeline.run(buffer, tools, config) do
       {:ok, msg, _updated_buffer, _env} ->
          {:ok, msg.content}
       error ->
          error
     end
  end

  @doc """
  Generates text embeddings.
  Delegates to ACN.AI.Embedder.
  """
  def embed(text), do: Embedder.embed(text)

end

Folder: lib/acn/observability
