defmodule ACN.Agents.Actuator.ContractVerifier do
  @moduledoc """
  Phase 3: Artifact Contract Verifier.

  Verifies that a leaf node has produced all the files it was contracted to create
  (as declared in the `file_manifest` from the Blueprint phase).

  Called by the Controller before emitting a success signal, ensuring that nodes
  cannot "complete" without having physically created their committed outputs.
  """

  require Logger

  @doc """
  Verifies that all files in `file_manifest` exist within `working_path`.

  ## Parameters
  - `working_path`: The absolute directory the node was bound to.
  - `file_manifest`: List of relative file paths the node was contracted to produce.

  ## Returns
  - `:ok` — All contracted files are present.
  - `{:error, missing_files}` — One or more files are missing. Returns the list.
  - `:skip` — No manifest was provided; verification is skipped (legacy compatibility).
  """
  @spec verify(String.t(), list(String.t())) :: :ok | {:error, list(String.t())} | :skip
  def verify(_working_path, nil), do: :skip
  def verify(_working_path, []), do: :skip
  def verify(working_path, file_manifest) when is_list(file_manifest) do
    base_dir = 
      if Path.extname(working_path) != "" do
        Path.dirname(working_path)
      else
        working_path
      end

    missing =
      file_manifest
      |> Enum.reject(fn relative_path ->
        abs_path = Path.join(base_dir, relative_path)
        basename_path = Path.join(base_dir, Path.basename(relative_path))
        File.exists?(abs_path) or File.exists?(basename_path)
      end)

    if Enum.empty?(missing) do
      Logger.info("ContractVerifier: All #{length(file_manifest)} contracted files verified in #{base_dir}.")
      :ok
    else
      Logger.error("""
      ContractVerifier: CONTRACT VIOLATION in #{base_dir}.
      Node declared it would produce #{length(file_manifest)} files but #{length(missing)} are MISSING:
      #{Enum.map_join(missing, "\n", fn f -> "  ✗ #{Path.join(base_dir, f)}" end)}
      """)
      {:error, missing}
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Builds a feedback string for the AI agent describing which files are missing.
  Used to inject into a retry prompt so the agent knows exactly what to produce.
  """
  @spec build_feedback(String.t(), list(String.t())) :: String.t()
  def build_feedback(working_path, missing_files) do
    base_dir = 
      if Path.extname(working_path) != "" do
        Path.dirname(working_path)
      else
        working_path
      end

    file_list = Enum.map_join(missing_files, "\n", fn f -> "  - #{Path.join(base_dir, f)}" end)
    """
    [CONTRACT VIOLATION]: Your task is not complete. The following files were declared in your blueprint \
    but were NOT created:
    #{file_list}

    You MUST create ALL missing files before your task can be considered complete. \
    Use the write_to_file tool for each missing file.
    """
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: sandbox.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule ACN.Agents.Actuator.Sandbox do
  @moduledoc """
  Manages the lifecycle of the Firecracker MicroVM.
  Acts as the "Function-as-a-Service" provider.
  """
  require Logger
  alias ACN.Actuator.RustlerBridge

  @doc """
  Executes a command inside the isolated Sandbox.
  1. Boot VM
  2. Run Command
  3. Teardown
  """
  def run(command, args, opts \\ []) do
    if Application.get_env(:acn, :simulation_mode, false) do
      run_simulation(command, args, opts)
    else
      run_firecracker(command, args, opts)
    end
  end

  defp run_firecracker(command, args, _opts) do
    # 1. Prepare Infrastructure
    uuid = UUID.uuid4()
    socket_path = "/tmp/acn_#{uuid}.socket"
    
    # 2. Spawn Firecracker
    # We must ensure previous socket is gone
    File.rm(socket_path)
    
    port = spawn_firecracker(socket_path)
    Logger.debug("Sandbox: VM #{uuid} spawned on #{socket_path}")

    try do
      # 3. Bridge Call (Blocking)
      # The NIF handles the connect/boot/exec sequence
      case RustlerBridge.run_cmd(socket_path, command, args) do
        {res, stdout, stderr, code} when res in [:ok] ->
           %{stdout: stdout, stderr: stderr, exit_code: code}
           
        {:error, reason} ->
           Logger.error("Sandbox NIF Error: #{reason}")
           %{stdout: "", stderr: reason, exit_code: -1}
      end
    rescue
      e -> 
        Logger.error("Sandbox Exception: #{inspect(e)}")
        %{stdout: "", stderr: "Internal Sandbox Error", exit_code: -1}
    after
      # 4. Cleanup
      terminate_port(port)
      File.rm(socket_path)
      File.rm("#{socket_path}.vsock") 
    end
  end

  defp run_simulation(command, args, opts) do
    Logger.warning("Sandbox: RUNNING LOCAL (Simulation Mode). Command: #{command}")
    
    # Concrete Implementation: Run on Host System
    # Security Warning: This runs with the same permissions as the BEAM node.
    do_run_simulation(command, args, opts)
  end
  
  defp do_run_simulation(command, args, opts) do
    full_command = if args == [], do: command, else: command <> " " <> Enum.join(args, " ")
    
    spawn_opts = 
      if opts[:cd] do
        [cd: opts[:cd], stderr_to_stdout: false]
      else
        [stderr_to_stdout: false]
      end

    case System.shell(full_command, spawn_opts) do
      {output, 0} -> 
        %{stdout: output, stderr: "", exit_code: 0}
      {output, code} -> 
        %{stdout: "", stderr: output, exit_code: code}
    end
  rescue
    e in ErlangError ->
      if e.original == :enoent and command == "python" do
        Logger.info("Sandbox: 'python' not found, falling back to 'python3'...")
        do_run_simulation("python3", args, opts)
      else
        %{stdout: "", stderr: "Local Execution Failed: #{inspect(e)}", exit_code: -1}
      end
    e -> 
       %{stdout: "", stderr: "Local Execution Failed: #{inspect(e)}", exit_code: -1}
  end

  defp spawn_firecracker(socket_path) do
    executable = System.find_executable("firecracker") || "firecracker"
    
    # Start Firecracker listening on UDS
    Port.open({:spawn_executable, executable}, [
      :binary, 
      :exit_status, 
      args: ["--api-sock", socket_path]
    ])
  end

  defp terminate_port(port) do
    if port do
      Port.close(port) 
      # Port.close sends SIGTERM/KILL to linked process
    end
  end
end

defmodule ACN.Actuator.Hardware.Adapter do
  @callback gpio_write(pin :: integer(), state :: :high | :low) :: :ok | {:error, term()}
  @callback gpio_read(pin :: integer()) :: {:ok, boolean()} | {:error, term()}
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: circuits_gpio.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule ACN.Actuator.Hardware.CircuitsGPIO do
  @behaviour ACN.Actuator.Hardware.Adapter
  require Logger

  # Only compile this on systems where Circuits.GPIO is available/safe
  # Or rely on the fact it's a NIF that might fail to load on non-Linux.
  # But the code itself is just Elixir.

  @impl true
  def gpio_write(pin, state) do
    val = if state == :high, do: 1, else: 0
    
    # Stateless open-write-close pattern for simplicity
    with {:ok, gpio} <- Circuits.GPIO.open(pin, :output),
         :ok <- Circuits.GPIO.write(gpio, val) do
      Circuits.GPIO.close(gpio)
      :ok
    else
      {:error, reason} ->
        Logger.error("GPIO Write Error (Pin #{pin}): #{inspect(reason)}")
        {:error, reason}
    end
  end

  @impl true
  def gpio_read(pin) do
    with {:ok, gpio} <- Circuits.GPIO.open(pin, :input),
         {:ok, val} <- Circuits.GPIO.read(gpio) do
      Circuits.GPIO.close(gpio)
      {:ok, val == 1}
    else
      {:error, reason} ->
        Logger.error("GPIO Read Error (Pin #{pin}): #{inspect(reason)}")
        {:error, reason}
    end
  end
end

defmodule ACN.Actuator.Hardware.Simulated do
  @behaviour ACN.Actuator.Hardware.Adapter

  def gpio_write(pin, state) do
     Logger.info("[HARDWARE SIMULATION] GPIO Pin #{pin} set to #{inspect(state)}")
     :ok
  end

  def gpio_read(pin) do
     Logger.info("[HARDWARE SIMULATION] GPIO Pin #{pin} read.")
     # Simulate random input or fixed
     {:ok, false}
  end
end

------------------------------------------------------------
File: state_backup.ex

defmodule ACN.Infrastructure.StateBackup do
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  @backup_dir "priv/data/backups"

  # --- Public API ---

  def start_link(_opts \\ []) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, [], name: __MODULE__)
  end

  def backup(agent_id, state, node_id \\ "global") do
    GenServer.cast(__MODULE__, {:backup, agent_id, state, node_id})
  end

  def restore(agent_id, node_id \\ "global") do
    GenServer.call(__MODULE__, {:restore, agent_id, node_id})
  end

  # --- Server Callbacks ---

  @impl true
  def init(_) do
    File.mkdir_p!(@backup_dir)
    {:ok, %{}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:backup, agent_id, state, node_id}, state_map) do
    filename = "#{node_id}_#{agent_id}.bin"
    path = Path.join(@backup_dir, filename)
    binary = :erlang.term_to_binary(state)
    File.write!(path, binary)
    {:noreply, state_map}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call({:restore, agent_id, node_id}, _from, state_map) do
    filename = "#{node_id}_#{agent_id}.bin"
    path = Path.join(@backup_dir, filename)

    result =
      if File.exists?(path) do
         try do
           binary = File.read!(path)
           term = :erlang.binary_to_term(binary)
           {:ok, term}
         rescue
           _e -> {:error, :corrupt_backup}
         end
      else
         {:error, :not_found}
      end

    {:reply, result, state_map}
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: memory_client.ex

defmodule ACN.Infrastructure.MemoryClient do
  @moduledoc """
  Client for the remote Associative Cognitive Memory System (CMS).
  
  This module acts as the bridge between the ACN (Agent/Client) and the 
  CMS (Brain/Server). It communicates exclusively via the CMS REST API.
  
  It is critical for the Supervisor's 'Wisdom' phase during Node Genesis.
  """
  require Logger
  alias ACN.Core.Artifacts.DBC

  # Default configuration with safety fallbacks
  defp config do
    config = Application.get_env(:acn, :memory, [])
    base_url = config[:cms_url] || "http://localhost:4000"
    api_key = config[:cms_api_key]

    [
      base_url: base_url, 
      headers: build_headers(api_key),
      # Critical: Prevent Node Genesis from hanging if ACMS is down
      receive_timeout: 10000, 
      connect_options: [timeout: 5000]
    ]
  end

  defp build_headers(nil), do: [{"content-type", "application/json"}, {"x-agent-id", "root"}]
  defp build_headers(_key), do: [{"content-type", "application/json"}, {"x-agent-id", "root"}]

  @doc """
  Ingests a Digital Birth Certificate (DBC) into the remote CMS.
  """
  def ingest(%DBC{} = dbc) do
    conf = config()
    url = "#{conf[:base_url]}/api/v1/ingest"

    # Map DBC to CMS Payload
    payloads = [%{"type" => "text", "content" => dbc.fact_text}]
    payloads = 
      if dbc.source_code && dbc.source_code != "" do
        payloads ++ [%{"type" => "code", "content" => dbc.source_code}]
      else
        payloads
      end
    
    request_body = %{
      "fact_text" => dbc.fact_text,
      "agent_id" => "root", 
      "description_payloads" => payloads,
      "provenance" => Map.merge(dbc.provenance_data || %{}, %{
        "verifier_id" => dbc.verifier_id,
        "artifact_hash" => dbc.artifact_hash,
        "origin_timestamp" => DateTime.to_iso8601(dbc.timestamp),
        "full_path" => dbc.full_path,
        "filename" => dbc.filename
      }),
      "acls" => %{
        "read" => ["public", "root", "system"],
        "write" => ["root", "system"]
      }
    }

    # Merge config options for timeout
    req_options = Keyword.drop(conf, [:base_url, :headers])
    
    case Req.post(url, [json: request_body, headers: conf[:headers]] ++ req_options) do
      {:ok, %Req.Response{status: 201, body: %{"node_id" => id}}} ->
        Logger.info("CMS Ingest Success. Node ID: #{id}")
        {:ok, id}

      {:ok, %Req.Response{status: 202, body: %{"meta_node_id" => id, "status" => "conflict_resolved"}}} ->
        Logger.info("CMS Ingest: Conflict Resolved via Dialectics. Meta-Node ID: #{id}")
        {:ok, id}

      {:ok, %Req.Response{status: status, body: body}} ->
        Logger.error("CMS Rejected Ingest (#{status}): #{inspect(body)}")
        {:error, :cms_rejection}

      {:error, reason} ->
        Logger.error("CMS Network Failure: #{inspect(reason)}")
        {:error, :network_failure}
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Performs a semantic search against the remote CMS.
  Used by SupervisorAgent to fetch 'Wisdom' before Identity Determination.
  """
  def query(query_text, limit \\ 5)

  def query(query_text, _limit) when is_nil(query_text) or query_text == "" do
    Logger.warning("MemoryClient: Aborting query with empty text.")
    {:ok, []}
  end

  def query(query_text, limit) do
    # Use the Limiter to serialize and rate-limit queries
    ACN.Infrastructure.ACMSLimiter.query(query_text, limit)
  end

  @doc """
  Internal execution of the query. Should generally be called via query/2 (Limiter).
  """
  def execute_query(query_text, limit) do
    conf = config()
    url = "#{conf[:base_url]}/api/v1/query"

    request_body = %{
      "query_text" => query_text,
      "max_results" => limit,
      "min_relevance" => 0.6,
      "agent_id" => "root",
      "embedding_model" => "all-MiniLM-L6-v2",
      "reasoning_mode" => "normal",
      "system_mode" => "normal"
    }

    req_options = Keyword.drop(conf, [:base_url, :headers])

    case Req.post(url, [json: request_body, headers: conf[:headers]] ++ req_options) do
      {:ok, %Req.Response{status: 200, body: %{"results" => results}}} ->
        # Return raw results to preserve full context (Wisdom)
        {:ok, results}

      {:ok, %Req.Response{status: status}} ->
        Logger.warning("CMS Query Failed with Status: #{status}")
        {:error, :query_failed}

      {:error, reason} ->
        # Silent failure preferred during Genesis to allow fallback heuristics
        Logger.warning("CMS Query Network Error: #{inspect(reason)}. Proceeding without wisdom.")
        {:ok, []} 
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Checks connectivity to the remote CMS.
  """
  def check_connection do
    conf = config()
    url = "#{conf[:base_url]}/api/v1/health/embedder"

    req_options = Keyword.drop(conf, [:base_url, :headers])

    case Req.get(url, [headers: conf[:headers]] ++ req_options) do
      {:ok, %Req.Response{status: 200}} -> :ok
      _ -> {:error, :cms_offline}
    end
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: acms_limiter.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule ACN.Infrastructure.ACMSLimiter do
  @moduledoc """
  GenServer that enforces a rate limit for ACMS queries.
  Ensures no more than 1 query per second is executed system-wide.
  """
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  defmodule State do
    defstruct last_request_at: 0, queue: :queue.new(), timer: nil
  end

  # --- Public API ---

  def start_link(opts \\ []) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, opts, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  @doc """
  Schedules and executes a query through the rate limiter.
  """
  def query(query_text, limit) do
    # Increased timeout to handle large backlogs, but non-blocking internal logic
    GenServer.call(__MODULE__, {:query, query_text, limit}, 600_000)
  end

  # --- Server Callbacks ---

  @impl true
  def init(_opts) do
    {:ok, %State{}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call({:query, query_text, limit}, from, state) do
    new_queue = :queue.in({from, query_text, limit}, state.queue)
    state = %{state | queue: new_queue}
    
    state = schedule_next(state)
    {:noreply, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:process_next, state) do
    case :queue.out(state.queue) do
      {{:value, {from, query_text, limit}}, remaining_queue} ->
        # Perform the actual query in a separate task to avoid blocking the limiter
        Task.start(fn ->
          result = ACN.Infrastructure.MemoryClient.execute_query(query_text, limit)
          GenServer.reply(from, result)
        end)

        new_state = %{state | queue: remaining_queue, last_request_at: System.system_time(:millisecond), timer: nil}
        {:noreply, schedule_next(new_state)}

      {:empty, _} ->
        {:noreply, %{state | timer: nil}}
    end
  end

  defp schedule_next(%State{timer: timer} = state) when is_reference(timer), do: state
  defp schedule_next(state) do
    if :queue.is_empty(state.queue) do
      state
    else
      now = System.system_time(:millisecond)
      elapsed = now - state.last_request_at
      wait_time = max(0, 1000 - elapsed)
      
      timer = Process.send_after(self(), :process_next, wait_time)
      %{state | timer: timer}
    end
  end
end

File: network_monitor.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule ACN.Infrastructure.NetworkMonitor do
  @moduledoc """
  Monitors cluster health and enforces "let it crash" on partition.
  If a parent node disconnects, its children on this node are terminated.
  """
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  def start_link(_opts) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, [], name: __MODULE__)
  end

  def init(_) do
    :net_kernel.monitor_nodes(true)
    {:ok, %{}}
  end

  def handle_info({:nodedown, node}, state) do
    Logger.warning("NetworkMonitor: Node Disconnected: #{node}. Initiating Partition Recovery.")
    
    # Prune Dead Remote Records (Optional but good for hygiene)
    prune_remote_records(node)

    # Terminate Orphans (Self-Termination)
    terminate_orphans_of(node)

    {:noreply, state}
  end

  def handle_info({:nodeup, node}, state) do
    Logger.info("NetworkMonitor: Node Connected: #{node}")
    {:noreply, state}
  end
  
  def handle_info(_msg, state), do: {:noreply, state}

  defp prune_remote_records(dead_node) do
    # Remove records from Mnesia that are on the dead node.
    # Note: In a real Mnesia cluster, mnesia handles split brain somewhat, 
    # but for our registry table (bag/set), we might want to manually clean up transient PIDs.
    # We will assume Mnesia handles the consistency or we just delete them to be sure.
    
    # Match pattern: {table, id, pid, node, role, parent, ts}
    # We want records where node == dead_node.
    
    transaction = fn ->
      :mnesia.select(:acn_node_registry, [
        {{:acn_node_registry, :"$1", :_, :"$2", :_, :_, :_}, 
         [{:==, :"$2", dead_node}], 
         [:"$1"]}
      ])
      |> Enum.each(fn id -> 
        :mnesia.delete({:acn_node_registry, id}) 
      end)
    end
    :mnesia.transaction(transaction)
  end

  defp terminate_orphans_of(dead_node) do
    # Find LOCAL processes whose parent is on dead_node.
    my_node = node()
    
    transaction = fn ->
      # 1. Find all potential parents on dead_node (IDs)
      # (Actually we deleted them in prune_remote_records, so we should have fetched them first or do it effectively)
      # But wait, if we delete them, we can't look them up as parents.
      # So we must find orphans based on the DEAD node logic before deleting?
      # Or:
      # Iterate all LOCAL records.
      # For each, check if parent_id resolves to a node that is dead_node.
      
      # Select local records: Node == my_node
      local_records = :mnesia.select(:acn_node_registry, [
        {{:acn_node_registry, :"$1", :"$2", :"$3", :_, :"$4", :_}, 
         [{:==, :"$3", my_node}], 
         [{{:"$1", :"$2", :"$4"}}] } # Return {id, pid, parent_id}
      ])
      
      local_records
      |> Enum.each(fn {id, pid, parent_id} ->
        if parent_id do
           # Check parent location
           case :mnesia.read(:acn_node_registry, parent_id) do
             [{_, ^parent_id, _, parent_node_addr, _, _, _}] ->
               if parent_node_addr == dead_node do
                 Logger.error("NetworkMonitor: Terminating Orphan Node #{id} (Parent on #{dead_node})")
                 Process.exit(pid, :kill)
               end
             [] ->
               # Parent missing? Maybe already deleted?
               # If parent is missing, strictly we are orphaned too.
               Logger.warning("NetworkMonitor: Node #{id} has missing parent. Terminating for safety.")
               Process.exit(pid, :kill)
           end
        end
      end)
    end
    
    :mnesia.transaction(transaction)
  end
end

defmodule ACN.Infrastructure.Workspace do
  @moduledoc """
  Manages filesystem isolation for ACN Nodes.
  Enforces that nodes can only write within their assigned jail.
  Handles atomic writes and backups.
  """
  require Logger
  alias ACN.Observability.Emitter

  # Default root if not configured
  @default_root "tmp/acn_workspaces"

  def root_path do
     Application.get_env(:acn, :workspace_root, @default_root)
     |> Path.expand()
  end

  @doc """
  Resolves the absolute path for a node's workspace.
  Each node gets its own isolated folder: workspace_root/node_id/
  
  This enables task isolation where each node's file operations are scoped
  to its own directory, preventing conflicts and enabling parallel execution.
  
  ## Examples
  
      iex> resolve("genesis_root_abc")
      "tmp/acn_workspaces/genesis_root_abc/"
      
      iex> resolve("task_xyz")
      "tmp/acn_workspaces/task_xyz/"
  """
  def resolve(node_id) do
    str_id = to_string(node_id)
    if Path.type(str_id) == :absolute do
      str_id
    else
      Path.join(root_path(), str_id)
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Initializes the workspace for a node.
  Creates directories: /src, /test, /logs, /artifacts, /.acn/history
  """
  def init_workspace(node_id) do
    root = resolve(node_id)

    dirs = [
      Path.join(root, "src"),
      Path.join(root, "test"),
      Path.join(root, "logs"),
      Path.join(root, "artifacts"),
      Path.join(root, ".acn/history")
    ]

    Enum.each(dirs, &File.mkdir_p!/1)

    {:ok, root}
  end

  def safe_path(node_id, relative_path, working_path \\ nil) do
    # Global absolute root for all ACN workspaces
    global_root = root_path()
    
    # 1. Determine the effective jail root.
    # If working_path is provided and is inside global_root, use it as the boundary.
    # IMPORTANT: working_path may be a FILE path (e.g. the legislator target_path).
    # Always normalise to a directory so sibling writes are not falsely rejected.
    node_root = resolve(node_id)
    
    jail_root =
      if working_path && String.starts_with?(working_path, global_root) do
        # If it's a regular file on disk, use its parent dir as the jail.
        # If it doesn't exist yet (pre-creation), also use dirname so that both
        # the file itself and siblings in the same directory are within bounds.
        if File.regular?(working_path) do
          Path.dirname(working_path)
        else
          # If it looks like a file (has an extension) treat it as file path too
          if Path.extname(working_path) != "" do
            Path.dirname(working_path)
          else
            working_path
          end
        end
      else
        node_root
      end
    
    # 2. Resolve the target path
    expanded = 
      if Path.type(relative_path) == :absolute do
         Path.expand(relative_path)
      else
         Path.expand(relative_path, jail_root)
      end
    
    # 3. Security Boundary Check
    if String.starts_with?(expanded, jail_root) do
       {:ok, expanded}
    else
       Logger.error("Workspace: SPATIAL JAIL VIOLATION. Attempted to access #{expanded} outside of #{jail_root}")
       {:error, :spatial_violation}
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Reads an artifact from the workspace.
  Enforces path safety.
  """
  def read_artifact(node_id, relative_path) do
    with {:ok, path} <- safe_path(node_id, relative_path) do
      File.read(path)
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Performs an atomic write with backup.
  """
  def write_atomic(node_id, relative_path, content, working_path \\ nil) do
     with :ok <- check_quota(node_id, byte_size(content)),
          # Fix 4C: Pass the working_path into safe_path to enforce strictly bounded spatial jailing
          {:ok, dest_path} <- safe_path(node_id, relative_path, working_path) do
         # Backup
         if File.exists?(dest_path) do
           root = resolve(node_id)
           history_dir = Path.join(root, ".acn/history")
           # Encure history dir exists (it should, but safe to check)
           File.mkdir_p!(history_dir)

           timestamp = System.system_time(:second)
           backup_name = "#{Path.basename(dest_path)}.#{timestamp}.bak"
           backup_path = Path.join(history_dir, backup_name)

           File.cp(dest_path, backup_path)
         end

         # Atomic Write
         tmp_path = dest_path <> ".tmp"
         File.mkdir_p!(Path.dirname(dest_path))
         File.write!(tmp_path, content)

         case File.rename(tmp_path, dest_path) do
           :ok -> 
             # HSG Layer 4: Spatial write & Vector Emission
             # For a full file rewrite, the diff is the whole new file content 
             # (or we could compute a real diff if we read the old, but for atomic overwrite, usually just capture the new content)
             diff_text = "--- ATOMIC OVERWRITE ---\n" <> String.slice(content, 0, 5000)
             
             # Calculate checksums
             checksum_after = :crypto.hash(:sha256, content) |> Base.encode16()
             # Normally we'd calculate checksum_before from backup, but we emit raw intent here
             
             Emitter.push_physical(%{
               file_path: relative_path,
               operation: :write,
               unified_diff: diff_text,
               checksum_before: nil,
               checksum_after: checksum_after
             })
             
             {:ok, dest_path}
           {:error, reason} -> {:error, reason}
         end

    end
  end
  def copy_artifact(node_id, source_rel, dest_rel) do
    with {:ok, source_path} <- safe_path(node_id, source_rel),
         {:ok, dest_path} <- safe_path(node_id, dest_rel) do
      
      File.mkdir_p!(Path.dirname(dest_path))
      
      case File.cp(source_path, dest_path) do
        :ok -> {:ok, dest_path}
        {:error, reason} -> {:error, reason}
      end
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Touches a file (creates if not exists, updates mtime).
  """
  def touch(node_id, relative_path, working_path \\ nil) do
    with {:ok, dest_path} <- safe_path(node_id, relative_path, working_path) do
       # Ensure parent exists
       File.mkdir_p!(Path.dirname(dest_path))
       
       case File.touch(dest_path) do
         :ok -> {:ok, dest_path}
         {:error, reason} -> {:error, reason}
       end
    end
  end

  defp check_quota(node_id, new_bytes) do
    root = resolve(node_id)
    # 500MB Limit
    limit = 500 * 1024 * 1024

    # Simple du -sb check (only on linux)
    current_size =
      case System.cmd("du", ["-sb", root], stderr_to_stdout: true) do
        {output, 0} ->
          case Integer.parse(output) do
            {bytes, _} -> bytes
            _ -> 0
          end
        _ -> 0
      end

    if current_size + new_bytes > limit do
      {:error, :quota_exceeded}
    else
      :ok
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Appends content to the end of a file safely.
  """
  def append(node_id, relative_path, content) do
    with :ok <- check_quota(node_id, byte_size(content)),
         {:ok, dest_path} <- safe_path(node_id, relative_path) do
      
      # Optional: Create backup before modification
      backup_file(node_id, dest_path)

      # Append operation
      case File.write(dest_path, content, [:append]) do
        :ok -> {:ok, dest_path}
        {:error, reason} -> {:error, reason}
      end
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Applies a Unified Diff to a physical file atomically.
  """
  def apply_patch(node_id, relative_path, diff_content) do
    with {:ok, dest_path} <- safe_path(node_id, relative_path),
         {:ok, current_content} <- File.read(dest_path) do

      # Use the shared Patch logic (refactored from SpecialistTools)
      case ACN.Core.Utils.Patcher.apply_patch(current_content, diff_content) do
        {:ok, new_content} ->
           # Use write_atomic to save the new version safely
           result = write_atomic(node_id, relative_path, new_content)
           
           # HSG Layer 4: Spatial write & Vector Emission for Patch
           checksum_before = :crypto.hash(:sha256, current_content) |> Base.encode16()
           checksum_after = :crypto.hash(:sha256, new_content) |> Base.encode16()
           
           Emitter.push_physical(%{
             file_path: relative_path,
             operation: :patch,
             unified_diff: diff_content,
             checksum_before: checksum_before,
             checksum_after: checksum_after
           })
           
           result
        
        {:error, reason} -> {:error, reason}
      end
    end
  end
  
  defp backup_file(node_id, dest_path) do
    if File.exists?(dest_path) do
      root = resolve(node_id)
      history_dir = Path.join(root, ".acn/history")
      # Ensure history dir exists
      File.mkdir_p!(history_dir)
      
      timestamp = System.system_time(:second)
      backup_name = "#{Path.basename(dest_path)}.#{timestamp}.bak"
      File.cp(dest_path, Path.join(history_dir, backup_name))
    end
  end
end

defmodule ACN.Infrastructure.Lattice do
  @moduledoc """
  Helper module for distributed lattice operations.
  Handles RPC calls and remote data fetching transparently.
  """
  require Logger

  @doc """
  Fetches artifact content from a specific node or from the global shared state (mocked for now).
  If `source_node` matches `node()`, it reads directly.
  Otherwise, it uses RPC.
  """
  def fetch_artifact(hash_or_path, nil), do: fetch_artifact(hash_or_path, node())
  def fetch_artifact(path, source_node) when is_binary(path) do
    if source_node == node() do
      File.read(path)
    else
      Logger.debug("Lattice: Fetching remote artifact from #{inspect(source_node)}: #{path}")
      
      case :rpc.call(source_node, File, :read, [path], 5000) do
        {:ok, content} -> {:ok, content}
        {:badrpc, reason} -> {:error, {:rpc_failed, reason}}
        {:error, reason} -> {:error, reason}
        error -> {:error, error}
      end
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Fetches the governance/context data from a parent node.
  """
  def fetch_ancestral_data(nil), do: {:error, :no_parent}
  def fetch_ancestral_data(parent_node) do
    if parent_node == node() do
      ACN.Agents.SupervisorAgent.get_public_context()
    else
      Logger.info("Lattice: Fetching ancestral data from #{inspect(parent_node)}")
      case :rpc.call(parent_node, ACN.Agents.SupervisorAgent, :get_public_context, [], 5000) do
        {:ok, data} -> {:ok, data}
        {:badrpc, reason} -> {:error, {:rpc_failed, reason}}
        error -> {:error, error}
      end
    end
  end
end

File: cluster_manager.ex

defmodule ACN.Infrastructure.ClusterManager do
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  @registry_table :acn_node_registry_v2

  def start_link(_opts) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, [], name: __MODULE__)
  end

  @impl true
  def init(_) do
    # 1. Initialize Mnesia
    ensure_mnesia_started()

    # 2. Join Cluster (if configured)
    join_cluster()

    # 3. Create Tables
    create_tables()

    # 4. Wait for Tables (with graceful fallback)
    wait_for_tables()

    {:ok, %{}}
  end

  defp ensure_mnesia_started do
    # Ensure Mnesia directory exists
    mnesia_dir = Application.get_env(:mnesia, :dir, 'priv/data/mnesia')
    File.mkdir_p!(to_string(mnesia_dir))

    # Ensure Mnesia is stopped before we potentially create schema
    :mnesia.stop()

    # Create schema if it doesn't exist
    case :mnesia.create_schema([node()]) do
      :ok -> Logger.info("Mnesia: Schema created")
      {:error, {_, {:already_exists, _}}} -> :ok
      {:error, reason} -> Logger.warning("Mnesia: Schema creation failed: #{inspect(reason)}")
    end

    # Start Mnesia
    :mnesia.start()
    Logger.info("Mnesia: Started")
  end

  defp join_cluster do
    seed_nodes = Application.get_env(:acn, :cluster)[:seed_nodes] || []

    # Filter out self
    targets = Enum.filter(seed_nodes, &(&1 != node()))

    if targets != [] do
       # Try to ping
       case Enum.find(targets, &Node.connect/1) do
         nil ->
           Logger.warning("Cluster: No seed nodes reachable via Distributed Erlang.")

         target ->
           Logger.info("Cluster: Connected to seed node #{target}")
           case :mnesia.change_config(:extra_db_nodes, [target]) do
             {:ok, _} ->
               Logger.info("Cluster: Mnesia joined cluster via #{target}")
               :mnesia.add_table_copy(:schema, node(), :disc_copies)
             error ->
               Logger.error("Cluster: Failed to join Mnesia: #{inspect(error)}")
           end
       end
    end
  end

  defp create_tables do
    # Check if table already exists
    existing_tables = :mnesia.system_info(:tables)

    if @registry_table in existing_tables do
      Logger.info("Mnesia: Table #{@registry_table} already exists")
      if Application.get_env(:acn, :clean_start) do
        Logger.info("ClusterManager: Deleting table #{@registry_table} (clean_start=true)")
        :mnesia.delete_table(@registry_table)
        Process.sleep(500)
        # Create it again immediately
        create_registry_table()
      end
    else
      create_registry_table()
    end
  end

  defp create_registry_table do
    clean_start = Application.get_env(:acn, :clean_start)

    copies_opts = if clean_start do
      # RAM copies ensure no old data persists
      [ram_copies: [node()]]
    else
      [disc_copies: [node()]]
    end

    result = :mnesia.create_table(@registry_table, [
      attributes: [:id, :pid, :node_address, :role, :parent_id, :created_at, :metadata]
    ] ++ copies_opts)

    case result do
      {:atomic, :ok} -> Logger.info("Mnesia: Table #{@registry_table} created (RAM: #{clean_start})")
      {:aborted, {:already_exists, _}} -> :ok
      {:aborted, reason} -> Logger.warning("Mnesia: Table creation failed: #{inspect(reason)}")
    end
  end

  defp wait_for_tables do
    case :mnesia.wait_for_tables([@registry_table], 5_000) do
      :ok ->
        Logger.info("Mnesia: Tables ready")
        :ok
      {:timeout, tables} ->
        Logger.warning("Mnesia: Table wait timed out for #{inspect(tables)}, proceeding anyway")
        :ok
      {:error, reason} ->
        Logger.warning("Mnesia: Table wait failed: #{inspect(reason)}, proceeding anyway")
        :ok
    end
  end

  # --- Cluster Scheduling API ---

  @doc """
  Selects the least stressed node in the cluster for spawning a new process.
  Prefers local node if the stress difference is minimal (< 10%).
  """
  def select_best_target do
    # 1. Get all nodes (connected + self)
    all_nodes = [node() | Node.list()]
    
    # 2. Poll Metrics (Parallel or Sequential? Sequential is safer for now)
    metrics = 
      Enum.map(all_nodes, fn n -> 
         poll_node_metrics(n)
      end)
    
    # 3. Sort by Stress (Ascending)
    sorted = Enum.sort_by(metrics, fn {_n, stress, _mem} -> stress end)
    
    # 4. Apply Affinity Logic
    # sorted is [{node, stress, mem}, ...]
    [{best_node, best_stress, _} | _] = sorted
    
    local_metrics = Enum.find(metrics, fn {n, _, _} -> n == node() end)
    {local_node, local_stress, _} = local_metrics || {node(), 100.0, 0}
    
    # Threshold check:
    # If best_node is remote, but local_stress - best_stress < 10, stick to local.
    threshold = Application.get_env(:acn, :cluster)[:migration_threshold] || 10.0
    
    final_target = 
      if best_node != local_node and (local_stress - best_stress) < threshold do
         local_node
      else
         best_node
      end
      
    Logger.debug("ClusterManager: Target Selection. Best: #{best_node} (#{best_stress}), Local: #{local_stress}. Picked: #{final_target}")
    
    final_target
  end

  defp poll_node_metrics(n) do
     # RPC call to that node's LiveWire
     # We use a timeout to avoid hanging spawn
     try do
       case :rpc.call(n, ACN.Agents.LiveWire, :get_remote_metrics, [], 1000) do
         {:ok, _n, stress, mem} -> {n, stress, mem}
         _ -> {n, 100.0, 0} # Treat unreachable/error as max stress
       end
     catch
       _, _ -> {n, 100.0, 0}
     end
  end
end

defmodule ACN.Infrastructure.Lattice.DeepContext do
  @moduledoc """
  The Six Pillars of Deep Context.
  Rigidly defined data structure that constitutes "Consciousness" for a node.
  """
  
  defmodule Rules do
    defstruct [
      master: [],      # System-wide safety (Immutable)
      ancestral: [],   # Union of Parent and Grandparent
      local: []        # Generated by current node's Legislator
    ]
  end

  defstruct [
    :blueprint,              # Pillar I: The technical narrative and architectural specification
    :file_manifest,          # Pillar I (Contract): Explicit list of relative file paths this node must produce
    :rules,                  # Pillar II: Master, Ancestral, and Local constraints
    :worksheets,             # Pillar III: Map of Parent, GP, GGP worksheet state
    :acms_results,           # Pillar IV: Semantic search results (Wisdom) - Full Node Bodies from CMS
    :task,                   # Pillar V: Atomic instruction
    :path,                   # Pillar VI: Absolute working directory
    :parent_node_id,         # Metadata: Parent Node ID for lattice functions
    :original_specification, # Pillar VII: Immutable root technical specification (full white paper)
    :specification_depth,    # Hierarchy depth for specification scoping (0=root, increments per generation)
    :specification_metadata  # Metadata about specification (version, timestamp, compliance requirements)
  ]

  @type t :: %__MODULE__{
    blueprint: String.t() | nil,
    file_manifest: list(String.t()) | nil,
    rules: %Rules{},
    worksheets: map(),
    acms_results: list(),
    task: String.t(),
    path: String.t(),
    parent_node_id: String.t() | nil,
    original_specification: String.t() | nil,
    specification_depth: non_neg_integer() | nil,
    specification_metadata: map() | nil
  }
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: rpc.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule ACN.Infrastructure.Lattice.RPC do
  @moduledoc """
  Wraps remote calls to ensure safety and strict timeouts.
  Prevents the Lattice from deadlocking on zombie nodes.
  """
  require Logger

  @default_timeout 5_000
  @max_payload_bytes 1_000_000 # 1MB

  @doc """
  Performs a safe GenServer call to a potentially remote PID.

  Args:
  - pid: The target PID (local or remote).
  - request: The message/request to send.
  - timeout: ms (default 5000).

  Returns:
  - {:ok, result}
  - {:error, :timeout}
  - {:error, :unreachable}
  - {:error, :payload_too_large}
  """
  def fast_call(pid, request, timeout \\ @default_timeout, opts \\ []) do
    if :erlang.term_to_binary(request) |> byte_size() > @max_payload_bytes do
      Logger.error("RPC Safety: Payload too large for #{inspect(pid)}")
      {:error, :payload_too_large}
    else
      try do
        result = GenServer.call(pid, request, timeout)
        {:ok, result}
      catch
        :exit, {:timeout, _} ->
          if !opts[:silent], do: Logger.warning("RPC Timeout calling #{inspect(pid)}")
          {:error, :timeout}

        :exit, {{:nodedown, _node}, _} ->
          if !opts[:silent], do: Logger.warning("RPC Node Down calling #{inspect(pid)}")
          {:error, :unreachable}

        :exit, {reason, _} ->
          # Silence expected :noproc (process died/finished) if requested
          if !opts[:silent] or reason != :noproc do
             Logger.warning("RPC Failed calling #{inspect(pid)}: #{inspect(reason)}")
          end
          {:error, :unreachable}
      end
    end
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: ancestry.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule ACN.Infrastructure.Lattice.NodeInfo do
  @moduledoc """
  Metadata about a single node in the lineage.
  """
  @type t :: %__MODULE__{
    id: String.t(),
    pid: pid(),
    node_address: atom(),
    role: atom()
  }
  defstruct [:id, :pid, :node_address, :role]
end

defmodule ACN.Infrastructure.Lattice.Ancestry do
  @moduledoc """
  Holds the resolved lineage of a node.
  """
  alias ACN.Infrastructure.Lattice.NodeInfo
  
  @type t :: %__MODULE__{
    parent: NodeInfo.t() | nil,
    grandparent: NodeInfo.t() | nil,
    great_grandparent: NodeInfo.t() | nil
  }
  defstruct [:parent, :grandparent, :great_grandparent]
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: lineage.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule ACN.Infrastructure.Lattice.Lineage do
  @moduledoc """
  Recursively resolves the ancestry of a node using Mnesia lookups and RPC validation.
  Handles conditional depth: stops traversal immediately if an ancestor is missing (Genesis/Root case).
  """
  require Logger
  alias ACN.Core.NodeRegistry
  alias ACN.Infrastructure.Lattice.{RPC, Ancestry, NodeInfo}

  @doc """
  Performs the Lineage Handshake with the Parent Node.
  Returns a map of ancestor PIDs: %{parent: pid, grandparent: pid, great_grandparent: pid}
  """
  def handshake(nil), do: %{parent: nil, parent_id: nil, grandparent: nil, grandparent_id: nil, great_grandparent: nil, great_grandparent_id: nil}
  def handshake(parent_pid) when is_pid(parent_pid) do
    case wait_for_handshake(parent_pid) do
      {:ok, lineage_map} -> lineage_map
      {:error, :parent_died} ->
        Logger.warning("Lineage: Parent #{inspect(parent_pid)} is already gone. Adopting orphan mode.")
        %{parent: nil, parent_id: nil, grandparent: nil, grandparent_id: nil, great_grandparent: nil, great_grandparent_id: nil}
    end
  end

  defp wait_for_handshake(parent_pid, attempt \\ 1) do
    # Fix 1B: Hard cap at 5 attempts. An unbounded retry loop was the root cause
    # of the :parent_died crash — the function would spin until OTP detected the
    # exit, then propagate it as a stop reason to the child GenServer.
    cond do
      not Process.alive?(parent_pid) ->
        Logger.warning("Lineage: Parent #{inspect(parent_pid)} is no longer alive. Orphan mode.")
        {:error, :parent_died}

      attempt > 5 ->
        Logger.warning("Lineage: Handshake retry limit reached for #{inspect(parent_pid)}. Orphan mode.")
        {:error, :parent_died}

      true ->
        try do
          case GenServer.call(parent_pid, :get_lineage, 5_000) do
            {:ok, lineage} ->
              {:ok, %{
                parent: lineage.parent_pid,
                parent_id: lineage.parent_id,
                grandparent: lineage.grandparent_pid,
                grandparent_id: lineage.grandparent_id,
                great_grandparent: lineage.great_grandparent_pid,
                great_grandparent_id: lineage.great_grandparent_id
              }}
            error ->
              Logger.warning("Lineage: Handshake returned error: #{inspect(error)}. Retrying (#{attempt}/5)...")
              Process.sleep(500)
              wait_for_handshake(parent_pid, attempt + 1)
          end
        catch
          :exit, reason ->
            Logger.warning("Lineage: Handshake RPC exit: #{inspect(reason)}. Retrying (#{attempt}/5)...")
            Process.sleep(500)
            wait_for_handshake(parent_pid, attempt + 1)
        end
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Resolves ancestors up to 3 levels deep based on the given Parent ID.
  If parent_id is nil, returns an empty Ancestry struct (Root Node).
  """
  def resolve_ancestors(nil), do: %Ancestry{}
  def resolve_ancestors(parent_id) do
    # 1. Resolve Parent
    case resolve_node(parent_id, "Parent") do
      {parent_info, grandparent_id} ->
        
        # 2. Resolve Grandparent (Conditional)
        {grandparent_info, great_grandparent_id} = 
          if grandparent_id do
            resolve_node(grandparent_id, "Grandparent")
          else
            {nil, nil}
          end

        # 3. Resolve Great-Grandparent (Conditional)
        {great_grandparent_info, _} = 
          if great_grandparent_id do
             resolve_node(great_grandparent_id, "Great-Grandparent")
          else
             {nil, nil}
          end

        %Ancestry{
          parent: parent_info,
          grandparent: grandparent_info,
          great_grandparent: great_grandparent_info
        }

      nil ->
        # Parent ID provided but not found/reachable -> Treat as Orphan/Root or Error based on strictness
        # For robustness, we log warning and return empty, allowing node to self-heal or start as root.
        Logger.warning("Lineage: Parent #{parent_id} defined but unreachable. Defaulting to Orphan state.")
        %Ancestry{}
    end
  end

  # --- Helper ---

  defp resolve_node(id, label) do
    Logger.debug("Lineage: Resolving #{label} (#{id})")
    
    # A. Look up in Registry to get location
    case NodeRegistry.lookup(id) do
      {:ok, %{pid: pid, node: node_addr, role: role, parent_id: _}} ->
         info = %NodeInfo{
           id: id,
           pid: pid,
           node_address: node_addr,
           role: role
         }

         # B. RPC to verify aliveness and get *its* parent (Next in line)
         # We ask the node for its parent_id to traverse up.
         case RPC.fast_call(pid, :get_parent_id, 2000) do
           {:ok, {:ok, next_ancestor_id}} ->
             {info, next_ancestor_id}
             
           {:ok, next_ancestor_id} when is_binary(next_ancestor_id) or is_nil(next_ancestor_id) ->
             {info, next_ancestor_id}

           {:error, reason} ->
             Logger.warning("Lineage: #{label} (#{id}) found in registry but unreachable via RPC: #{inspect(reason)}")
             nil
         end

      _ ->
         Logger.warning("Lineage: #{label} (#{id}) missing from Registry.")
         nil
    end
  end
end

defmodule ACN.Core.NodeMonitor do
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  @registry_table :acn_node_registry_v2

  def start_link(_opts) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, [], name: __MODULE__)
  end

  def init(_) do
    {:ok, %{}}
  end

  def monitor(pid, node_id) do
    GenServer.cast(__MODULE__, {:monitor, pid, node_id})
  end

  def handle_cast({:monitor, pid, node_id}, state) do
    Process.monitor(pid)
    # Store mapping ref -> node_id if needed, or rely on info msg?
    # DOWN msg contains ref and pid (object).
    # We map PID -> NodeID?
    # But if PID is reused (rare but possible with high churn)?
    # Use ref mapping in state: %{ref => node_id}

    # We assume 'monitor' returns a ref
    # Wait, Process.monitor returns ref.
    # But here we monitored inside handle_cast. We don't have the ref from caller.
    # We must store it.

    # Correction: We just monitored.
    # State update needed.
    {:noreply, Map.put(state, pid, node_id)}
  end

  def handle_info({:DOWN, _ref, :process, pid, _reason}, state) do
    case Map.fetch(state, pid) do
      {:ok, node_id} ->
        Logger.info("NodeMonitor: Detected death of #{node_id} (#{inspect(pid)}). Pruning registry.")

        # Prune Mnesia
        # Option A: Delete row
        # Option B: Mark as dead (update PID to nil?)
        # Directive says "remove or tag... as Dead".
        # Let's delete to keep table clean for now.
        transaction = fn ->
          # SAFE PRUNE: Only delete if the registry still points to THIS dead pid.
          # If it points to a new PID (restart), do NOT delete.
          case :mnesia.read(@registry_table, node_id) do
            [{_tab, ^node_id, ^pid, _addr, _role, _parent, _ts, _meta}] ->
               :mnesia.delete({@registry_table, node_id})
            
            _ -> 
               # Entry mismatch (already updated to new PID) or already gone.
               :ok
          end
        end
        :mnesia.transaction(transaction)

        {:noreply, Map.delete(state, pid)}

      :error ->
        # Unknown PID (maybe from before restart?)
        {:noreply, state}
    end
  end
end

defmodule ACN.Core.LatticeRegistry do
  @moduledoc """
  The Lattice Registry (The Live Map).
  
  Unlike a standard registry that maps names to PIDs, the Lattice mapping is semantic and path-aware:
  Feature Description <-> File Path <-> Module Name <-> PID
  
  It enables "Horizontal Incorporation" (Discovery) so nodes can find each other without imports.
  Uses ACMS (Associative Cognitive Memory System) as the General Memory backend.
  """
  
  use GenServer
  require Logger
  
  # Mnesia table name
  @table :acn_lattice_registry
  # ACMS HTTP endpoint
  @acms_base_url "http://localhost:4000/api/v1"
  
  defstruct [:path, :module, :exports, :pid, :metadata, :acms_node_id]

  # --- Client API ---

  def start_link(_opts) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, [], name: __MODULE__)
  end
  
  @doc """
  Registers a capability (Leaf Node).
  Commits to both Mnesia (fast local lookup) and ACMS (semantic search).
  """
  def register(path, module, exports, pid \\ nil, metadata \\ %{}) do
    GenServer.call(__MODULE__, {:register, path, module, exports, pid, metadata})
  end

  @doc """
  Finds a capability by exact path.
  """
  def lookup_by_path(path) do
    case :mnesia.dirty_read(@table, path) do
      [{_tbl, _path, module, exports, pid, meta, _acms_id}] ->
        {:ok, %__MODULE__{path: path, module: module, exports: exports, pid: pid, metadata: meta}}
      [] -> 
        {:error, :not_found}
    end
  end
  
  @doc """
  Finds a capability by module name.
  """
  def lookup_by_module(module) do
    match_spec = [{
      { :_, :"$1", :"$2", :_, :_, :_, :_ },
      [{:==, :"$2", module}],
      [:"$_"]
    }]
    
    case :mnesia.dirty_select(@table, match_spec) do
      [{_tbl, path, _mod, exports, pid, meta, _acms_id}] ->
        {:ok, %__MODULE__{path: path, module: module, exports: exports, pid: pid, metadata: meta}}
      [] -> 
        {:error, :not_found}
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Semantic Search using ACMS.
  Returns list of candidates matching the query.
  """
  def search(query_text) do
    body = Jason.encode!(%{
      query_text: query_text,
      agent_id: "root",
      max_results: 20,
      min_relevance: 0.5,
      embedding_model: "all-MiniLM-L6-v2",
      reasoning_mode: "normal",
      system_mode: "normal"
    })
    
    headers = [{"Content-Type", "application/json"}]
    
    case HTTPoison.post("#{@acms_base_url}/query", body, headers) do
      {:ok, %{status_code: 200, body: response_body}} ->
        case Jason.decode(response_body) do
          {:ok, %{"results" => results}} ->
            capabilities = Enum.map(results, fn r ->
              %{
                path: r["metadata"]["path"],
                module: r["metadata"]["module"],
                score: r["score"],
                fact: r["fact"]
              }
            end)
            {:ok, capabilities}
          _ -> {:error, :decode_failed}
        end
      {:error, reason} ->
        Logger.warn("ACMS query failed: #{inspect reason}")
        {:ok, []}
    end
  end

  # --- Server Callbacks ---

  @impl true
  def init(_) do
    Logger.info("Starting Lattice Registry with ACMS backend...")
    init_mnesia()
    {:ok, %{}}
  end
  
  @impl true
  def handle_call({:register, path, module, exports, pid, metadata}, _from, state) do
    # 1. Register in Mnesia (fast local lookup)
    transaction = fn ->
      # Initial write with nil acms_id, will update after ACMS responds
      :mnesia.write({@table, path, module, exports, pid, metadata, nil})
    end
    
    case :mnesia.transaction(transaction) do
      {:atomic, :ok} -> 
        Logger.info("Registered Capability: #{inspect module} at #{path}")
        
        # 2. Register in ACMS (semantic memory)
        spawn(fn -> register_to_acms(path, module, exports, metadata) end)
        
        {:reply, :ok, state}
      {:aborted, reason} -> 
        Logger.error("Failed to register capability: #{inspect reason}")
        {:reply, {:error, reason}, state}
    end
  end

  defp register_to_acms(path, module, exports, metadata) do
    fact_text = "Module #{inspect module} at #{path} with exports: #{inspect exports}"
    
    body = Jason.encode!(%{
      fact_text: fact_text,
      agent_id: "root",
      acls: %{
        read: ["public", "root", "system"],
        write: ["root", "system"]
      },
      provenance: %{source: "lattice_registry", trust_score: 1.0},
      description_payloads: [
        %{type: "object", object_type: "module_registration", data: %{
          path: path,
          module: to_string(module),
          exports: exports,
          metadata: metadata
        }}
      ]
    })
    
    headers = [{"Content-Type", "application/json"}, {"x-agent-id", "root"}]
    
    case HTTPoison.post("#{@acms_base_url}/ingest", body, headers) do
      {:ok, %{status_code: 201, body: response}} ->
        case Jason.decode(response) do
          {:ok, %{"node_id" => node_id}} ->
            Logger.info("ACMS ingestion successful: #{node_id}")
            # Update Mnesia with ACMS node_id
            update_acms_id(path, node_id)
          _ -> :ok
        end
      {:error, reason} ->
        Logger.warn("ACMS ingestion failed: #{inspect reason}")
    end
  end
  
  defp update_acms_id(path, acms_id) do
    transaction = fn ->
      case :mnesia.read(@table, path) do
        [{tbl, p, mod, exp, pid, meta, _}] ->
          :mnesia.write({tbl, p, mod, exp, pid, meta, acms_id})
        [] -> :ok
      end
    end
    :mnesia.transaction(transaction)
  end

  defp init_mnesia do
    case :mnesia.create_table(@table, [
      attributes: [:path, :module, :exports, :pid, :metadata, :acms_node_id], 
      disc_copies: [node()]
    ]) do
      {:atomic, :ok} -> :ok
      {:aborted, {:already_exists, _}} -> :ok
      other -> Logger.error("Mnesia table creation failed: #{inspect other}")
    end
  end
end

defmodule ACN.Core.NodeRegistry do
  @moduledoc """
  Distributed Registry using Mnesia.
  Maps Node UUID -> metadata (PID, Address, Role, Parent).
  Replaces local Elixir Registry.
  """
  require Logger

  @table :acn_node_registry_v2
  # @table :acn_node_registry


  # --- Public API ---

  def child_spec do
    # No process needed; Mnesia is the process.
    # We return an ignore spec to satisfy supervision tree requirements.
    %{
      id: __MODULE__,
      start: {Function, :identity, [:ignore]},
      type: :worker
    }
  end

  @doc """
  Registers a new node in the distributed registry.
  Must be called synchronously before the node logic starts (e.g. in SupervisorWrapper).
  """
  def register_node(id, pid, role, parent_id, metadata \\ %{}) do
    Logger.info("NodeRegistry.register_node called for #{inspect id}, pid: #{inspect pid} by #{inspect self()}")
    # Write to Mnesia
    node_addr = if pid, do: node(pid), else: node()

    row = {
      @table,
      id,       # Key
      pid,      # PID
      node_addr,# Address
      role,
      parent_id,
      System.system_time(:second),
      metadata
    }

    transaction = fn ->
      :mnesia.write(row)
    end

    case :mnesia.transaction(transaction) do
      {:atomic, :ok} -> :ok
      {:aborted, reason} -> {:error, reason}
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Updates metadata for an existing node.
  """
  def update_metadata(id, metadata_update) do
    transaction = fn ->
      case :mnesia.read(@table, id) do
        [{table, ^id, pid, node, role, parent, created, old_meta}] ->
          new_meta = Map.merge(old_meta || %{}, metadata_update)
          :mnesia.write({table, id, pid, node, role, parent, created, new_meta})
        [] ->
          :mnesia.abort(:not_found)
      end
    end

    case :mnesia.transaction(transaction) do
      {:atomic, :ok} -> :ok
      err -> err
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Updates the parent_id for an existing node.
  """
  def update_parent(id, parent_id) do
    transaction = fn ->
      case :mnesia.read(@table, id) do
        [{table, ^id, pid, node, role, _old_parent, created, meta}] ->
          :mnesia.write({table, id, pid, node, role, parent_id, created, meta})
        [] ->
          :mnesia.abort(:not_found)
      end
    end

    case :mnesia.transaction(transaction) do
      {:atomic, :ok} -> :ok
      err -> err
    end
  end

  # --- Data Access ---

  @doc """
  Looks up a node by ID.
  Returns {:ok, %{pid: pid, node: address, ...}} or {:error, :not_found}.
  """
  def lookup(id) do
    # Dirty read for speed (RAM access)
    result = case :mnesia.dirty_read(@table, id) do
      [{_table, _id, pid, node_addr, role, parent_id, _ts, metadata}] ->
        {:ok, %{
          pid: pid,
          node: node_addr,
          role: role,
          parent_id: parent_id,
          metadata: metadata
        }}

      # Handle legacy/migration case where record might have 6 elements?
      # Mnesia might return the tuple as is.
      # If schema changed but data didn't transform, reading might crash or return old tuple.
      # We assume clean slate or successful migration for now.

      [] -> {:error, :not_found}
    end
    Logger.debug("NodeRegistry.lookup(#{inspect id}) -> #{inspect result}")
    result
  end

  # Helper for existing code compatibility?
  # Existing code calls NodeRegistry.lookup(name, id)
  # Only 'id' matters now. 'name' (:supervisor etc) is implicit in context.
  # We should support simple lookup by id.

  # Handling Via Tuple:
  # Older code: name: via_tuple(node_id)
  # GenServer calls Registry.
  # We can't easily shim Mnesia into via_tuple logic without a custom registry module implementation
  # that implements the :via callbacks (register_name, whereis_name, etc).
  # Implementing the Via behavior for Mnesia is the best way to keep existing code working!

  # --- Elixir Registry (Via) Behavior ---

  def whereis_name({_name_atom, id}) do
    # Logger.debug("whereis_name(#{inspect id})")
    # We ignore the atom name (:supervisor) and route purely by ID
    case lookup(id) do
      {:ok, %{pid: pid}} when is_pid(pid) ->
        if Process.alive?(pid) do
          pid
        else
          :undefined
        end

      _ -> :undefined
    end
  end

  def register_name({name_atom, id}, pid) do
    Logger.debug("NodeRegistry: register_name called for #{inspect name_atom}/#{inspect id} by PID #{inspect pid}")
    transaction = fn ->
      case :mnesia.read(@table, id) do
        [{table, ^id, old_pid, _addr, role, parent, created, meta}] ->
           # Check if valid PID and Alive
           is_alive = is_pid(old_pid) && Process.alive?(old_pid)

           if is_alive and old_pid != pid do
             Logger.error("CONFLICT: ID #{inspect(id)} owned by ALIVE #{inspect(old_pid)}. New: #{inspect(pid)}")
             :mnesia.abort(:already_registered)
           else
             Logger.debug("Overwriting #{inspect id} to #{inspect pid}")
             :mnesia.write({table, id, pid, node(pid), role, parent, created, meta})
           end

        [] ->
           Logger.debug("Writing NEW #{inspect id} -> #{inspect pid}")
           :mnesia.write({@table, id, pid, node(pid), :leaf, nil, System.system_time(:second), %{}})
      end
    end

    case :mnesia.transaction(transaction) do
      {:atomic, :ok} ->
        ACN.Core.NodeMonitor.monitor(pid, id)
        :yes
      {:aborted, :already_registered} ->
        :no
      other ->
        Logger.error("Register transaction failed: #{inspect other}")
        :no
    end
  end

  def unregister_name({_name_atom, id}) do
    :mnesia.dirty_delete(@table, id)
  end

  def send({_name_atom, id}, msg) do
    case lookup(id) do
      {:ok, %{pid: pid}} -> Kernel.send(pid, msg)
      _ -> :badarg
    end
  end

  # Allow simple ID usage too via {:via, ACN.Core.NodeRegistry, {name, id}}
  def via_tuple(name, id \\ nil) do
    {:via, __MODULE__, {name, id}}
  end
end

defmodule ACN.Core.AuditBuffer do
  @moduledoc """
  HSG Phase 1 — Legacy buffer shim (flat-file flush disabled).

  The AuditBuffer GenServer remains in the supervision tree for backward
  compatibility with any call-site that invokes `AuditBuffer.log/2`. However,
  the periodic flat-file flush is now a no-op.

  Physical file output is handled by `ACN.Observability.ShadowWriter` (Phase 4),
  which writes structured JSONL into the `.shadow` overlay alongside generated files.

  This module may be removed entirely once all direct callers are migrated.
  """
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  # --- Public API ---

  def start_link(opts \\ []) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, opts, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  def log(node_id, message) do
    # Route to debug — ShadowWriter is now the authoritative log sink.
    Logger.debug("[AuditBuffer] node=#{node_id} #{message}")
  end

  # --- Server Callbacks ---

  @impl true
  def init(_opts) do
    # No flat-file directories created; ShadowWriter owns the filesystem write path.
    Logger.debug("AuditBuffer: Started (flat-file flush disabled — HSG ShadowWriter active).")
    {:ok, %{}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:log, node_id, message}, state) do
    Logger.debug("[AuditBuffer] node=#{node_id} #{message}")
    {:noreply, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast(_msg, state), do: {:noreply, state}

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:periodic_flush, state) do
    # No-op. ShadowWriter handles filesystem output.
    {:noreply, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(_msg, state), do: {:noreply, state}
end

defmodule ACN.Core.Worksheet do
  @moduledoc """
  Formalizes the Agent's Memory/Workspace.
  Consists of typed Slots containing artifacts (code, plans, text).
  """
  
  defmodule Slot do
    @derive {Jason.Encoder, only: [:content, :type, :hash, :timestamp]}
    @type t :: %__MODULE__{
      content: any(),
      type: atom(),    # :code | :plan | :text | :rule
      hash: String.t(),
      timestamp: integer()
    }
    defstruct [:content, :type, :hash, :timestamp]
    
    def new(content, type) do
      %__MODULE__{
        content: content,
        type: type,
        hash: :crypto.hash(:sha256, inspect(content, limit: :infinity)) |> Base.encode16(),
        timestamp: System.system_time(:second)
      }
    end
  end

  @type t :: %__MODULE__{
    slots: %{String.t() => Slot.t()}
  }
  defstruct slots: %{}

  def new do
    %__MODULE__{slots: %{}}
  end
  
  def put(%__MODULE__{} = ws, key, content, type \\ :text) do
    slot = Slot.new(content, type)
    new_slots = Map.put(ws.slots, key, slot)
    %{ws | slots: new_slots}
  end

  def get(%__MODULE__{} = ws, key) do
    Map.get(ws.slots, key)
  end

  def to_map(%__MODULE__{} = ws) do
    # Returns a simple map of {key => content} for easier consumption
    Enum.reduce(ws.slots, %{}, fn {k, slot}, acc ->
      Map.put(acc, k, slot.content)
    end)
  end

  def save(%__MODULE__{} = ws, base_path) do
    if base_path do
      # If the path points to a file (e.g., math.js), we want to save the worksheet in its parent directory
      real_base_path = if Path.extname(base_path) != "", do: Path.dirname(base_path), else: base_path
      file_path = Path.join([real_base_path, ".acn", "worksheet.json"])
      
      # Ensure directory exists (redundant safety)
      File.mkdir_p!(Path.dirname(file_path))
      
      case Jason.encode(ws.slots, pretty: true) do
        {:ok, json} -> 
           File.write(file_path, json)
           :ok
        {:error, reason} ->
           {:error, reason}
      end
    else
      {:error, :no_path}
    end
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: router.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule ACN.Router do
  @moduledoc """
  The Router is a library composed into every agent.
  It handles Horizontal Wiring and enforces the Security Protocol.
  """
  require Logger

  defmodule SecurityProtocolError do
    defexception message: "Security Protocol Violation"
  end

  defmodule State do
    defstruct [
      agent_role: nil,          # :specialist, :controller, etc.
      node_id: "root",          # UNIQUE ID for this logical node
      local_directory: %{},     # Map of sibling PIDs: %{actuator: #PID<...>}
      uplink: nil,              # Parent Node PID
      downlinks: %{},           # Map of Child Node PIDs
      sideways_bus: nil,        # Phoenix.PubSub topic for Nervous System
      merge_slot_id: nil        # ID of the slot in parent's NodeSheet
    ]
  end

  @doc """
  Secure Dispatch Logic: The gatekeeper.
  Function: route(payload, destination, state)
  """
  def route(payload, destination, %State{} = state) do
    case destination do
      {:local, role} ->
        # 1. Lookup with dynamic refresh
        result = 
          case Map.get(state.local_directory, role) do
            pid when is_pid(pid) -> {pid, state}
            nil -> lookup_and_refresh(role, state)
          end

        case result do
          {pid, updated_state} when is_pid(pid) ->
            # 2. Security Constraint
            # The Supervisor is exempted from token validation when talking to Actuator 
            # (e.g. for lattice_merge/physical topology setup)
            if role == :actuator and state.agent_role != :supervisor do
              validate_token!(payload)
            end

            # 3. Dispatch
            sender_role = state.agent_role
            GenServer.cast(pid, {:routed_message, sender_role, payload})
            {:ok, updated_state}

          _ ->
            Logger.warning("Routing failed: Local role #{inspect(role)} not found in local_directory.")
            {:error, :not_found}
        end

      {:uplink, _} ->
        route_uplink(payload, state)

      {:downlink, child_id} ->
        route_downlink(payload, child_id, state)

      {:sideways, topic} ->
        # Phoenix.PubSub Wiring
        target_topic = 
          case topic do
            :pain_signal -> "acn:monitor:trauma"
            :internal -> "acn:node_internal:#{state.node_id}"
            _ -> "acn:sideways:#{topic}"
          end
        
        Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(ACN.PubSub, target_topic, {:routed_sideways, state.agent_role, payload})

      {:upstream, :merge} ->
        # Wrap payload for Lattice Protocol
        merge_payload = {:lattice_merge, [slot_id: state.merge_slot_id, data: payload]}
        route_uplink(merge_payload, state)

      {:external, :acms} ->
        ACN.Infrastructure.MemoryClient.query(payload)

      {:external, :acms_ingest} ->
        ACN.Infrastructure.MemoryClient.ingest(payload)

      {:self, _} ->
        # Internal loopback for agents (e.g. Specialists morphing/debugging)
        GenServer.cast(self(), payload)

      _ ->
        Logger.error("Unknown routing destination: #{inspect(destination)}")
        {:error, :invalid_destination}
    end
  end

  defp validate_token!(payload) do
    token =
      cond do
        is_map(payload) -> Map.get(payload, :token) || Map.get(payload, "token")
        is_tuple(payload) -> try_extract_token_from_tuple(payload)
        true -> nil
      end

    if is_nil(token) do
      Logger.error("Security Protocol Violation: Missing token for :actuator. Payload: #{inspect(payload)}")
      raise SecurityProtocolError, "Secure Dispatch Failed: Missing :token in payload for :actuator"
    end
  end

  defp try_extract_token_from_tuple(tuple) do
    # Convention: If it's {:perform_action, payload}, check payload
    case tuple do
      {:perform_action, %{token: t}} -> t
      {:perform_action, %{"token" => t}} -> t
      {:orchestrate_lifecycle, %{token: t}} -> t
      {:orchestrate_lifecycle, %{"token" => t}} -> t
      {:tests_provisioned, _work_id, _code, _cmd, t} -> t
      {:lattice_merge, _slot_id, _data, t} -> t
      {:merge_artifacts, _, t} -> t
      _ -> nil
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Legacy route/2 for backward compatibility.
  """
  def route(payload, destination) when not is_struct(destination, State) do
    case destination do
      {:local, agent_name} ->
        case ACN.Core.NodeRegistry.lookup(agent_name) do
          {:ok, %{pid: pid}} when is_pid(pid) ->
            GenServer.cast(pid, payload)
          _ ->
            Logger.warning("Routing failed: Local agent #{inspect(agent_name)} not found.")
        end

      {:sideways, topic} ->
        # Legacy PubSub fallback
        Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(ACN.PubSub, "acn:sideways:#{topic}", payload)

      _ ->
        Logger.error("Unknown routing destination.")
    end
  end

  defp route_uplink(payload, %State{uplink: pid}) when is_pid(pid) do
    GenServer.cast(pid, payload)
  end
  defp route_uplink(_, _) do
    Logger.warning("Routing failed: Uplink not available.")
  end

  defp route_downlink(payload, child_id, %State{downlinks: downlinks}) do
    case Map.get(downlinks, child_id) do
      pid when is_pid(pid) ->
        GenServer.cast(pid, payload)
      _ ->
        Logger.warning("Routing failed: Child #{inspect(child_id)} not found.")
    end
  end

  # --- Internal Discovery Logic ---

  def discover_local_agents(node_id \\ "root") do
    roles = [:controller, :actuator, :validator, :specialist, :task_manager, :supervisor, :live_wire, :synthesizer, :messenger]
    Enum.reduce(roles, %{}, fn role, acc ->
      # Scoped ID construction: Supervisor is the node_id, others are nodeid_role
      target_id = 
        if role == :supervisor do
          node_id
        else
          "#{node_id}_#{role}"
        end

      case ACN.Core.NodeRegistry.lookup(target_id) do
        {:ok, %{pid: pid}} -> Map.put(acc, role, pid)
        _ -> acc
      end
    end)
  end

  defp lookup_and_refresh(role, %State{} = state) do
    # Try scoped lookup
    target_id = 
      if role == :supervisor do
        state.node_id
      else
        "#{state.node_id}_#{role}"
      end

    case ACN.Core.NodeRegistry.lookup(target_id) do
      {:ok, %{pid: pid}} ->
        Logger.info("Router: Dynamically discovered local agent #{inspect(role)} -> #{inspect(pid)}")
        {pid, %{state | local_directory: Map.put(state.local_directory, role, pid)}}
      other ->
        Logger.warning("Router: Dynamic discovery FAILED for #{inspect(role)} in node #{state.node_id}. Registry returned: #{inspect(other)}")
        nil
    end
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: node_supervisor.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule ACN.Core.NodeSupervisor do
  use DynamicSupervisor
  require Logger

  def start_link(init_arg \\ []) do
    DynamicSupervisor.start_link(__MODULE__, init_arg, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  def init(_init_arg) do
    DynamicSupervisor.init(strategy: :one_for_one, max_restarts: 10, max_seconds: 60)
  end

  def start_child_node(args) do
    # 1. Ask ClusterManager for best target
    target_node = ACN.Infrastructure.ClusterManager.select_best_target()
    
    # 2. Dispatch
    if target_node == node() do
      spawn_local_child(args)
    else
      Logger.info("NodeSupervisor: Dispatching spawn to remote node #{target_node}")
      # RPC Call to spawn_local_child on remote node
      # We assume the code base is identical on all nodes.
      case :rpc.call(target_node, __MODULE__, :spawn_local_child, [args]) do
        {:ok, pid} -> {:ok, pid}
        error -> 
           Logger.error("NodeSupervisor: Remote spawn failed on #{target_node}: #{inspect(error)}. Fallback to local.")
           spawn_local_child(args)
      end
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Executes the actual DynamicSupervisor start_child on the local node.
  Can be called via RPC from a remote parent.
  """
  def spawn_local_child(args) do
    # args must contain keys like :task (which serves as ID), :task_type, :parent_node
    # args[:task] is usually an ID string "task_1"
    
    # We verify args has what we need, at least an ID
    if is_nil(args[:task]) and is_nil(args[:id]), do: raise "Cannot spawn child without :task or :id"

    # Support new ID key
    id = args[:task] || args[:id]
    parent_id = args[:parent_node] || args[:parent] # generic support
    
    # Legacy Registration (Only if not using LatticeRegistry self-registration?)
    # For now, we leave this for backward compatibility for old agents, 
    # but new agents register themselves in init().
    if args[:legacy_register] do
      role = if args[:task_type] == :manager, do: :intermediate, else: :leaf
      ACN.Core.NodeRegistry.register_node(id, nil, role, parent_id)
    end

    # 2. Spawn Child
    child_module = args[:child_module] || ACN.Agents.SupervisorAgent
    spec = {child_module, args}
    
    DynamicSupervisor.start_child(__MODULE__, spec)
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: profile_loader.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule ACN.Core.ProfileLoader do
  require Logger

  @doc """
  Loads and parses a YAML profile, supporting EEx variable injection.
  Args:
  - profile_name: String (filename without extension)
  - vars: Map of variables to inject into the template (default: %{})
  """
  def load(profile_name, vars \\ %{}) do
    # Resolve Path
    path = Application.app_dir(:acn, "priv/profiles/#{profile_name}.yaml")

    if File.exists?(path) do
      try do
        # 1. Read Raw String
        raw_yaml = File.read!(path)

        # 2. Evaluate EEx (Safety: binding vars)
        # Convert vars to atom keys for bindings
        atom_vars = Map.new(vars, fn {k, v} -> {String.to_atom(to_string(k)), v} end)

        # We bind variables as locals AND as 'assigns' to support @var syntax
        # We also add an 'indent' helper
        indent_fn = fn str, spaces ->
          prefix = String.duplicate(" ", spaces)

          # Safely coerce any type (map, struct, list, nil, etc.) to a printable string.
          # Using inspect/1 for non-string types avoids Protocol.UndefinedError and
          # guarantees the output is always a valid string.
          safe_str =
            cond do
              is_binary(str) -> str
              is_nil(str) -> ""
              is_map(str) or is_list(str) -> inspect(str, pretty: true, limit: :infinity)
              true -> to_string(str)
            end

          # Indent EVERY line (including line 0) so that injected content never
          # drops below the YAML block scalar's base indentation level and
          # accidentally terminates the scalar early.
          safe_str
          |> String.split("\n")
          |> Enum.map_join("\n", fn line -> "#{prefix}#{line}" end)
        end

        bindings = Map.to_list(atom_vars) ++ [assigns: atom_vars, indent: indent_fn]
        
        rendered_yaml = EEx.eval_string(raw_yaml, bindings)

        # 3. Parse YAML
        case YamlElixir.read_from_string(rendered_yaml) do
          {:ok, map} -> {:ok, map}
          {:error, reason} -> {:error, {:parse_error, reason}}
        end
      rescue
        e ->
          Logger.error("Failed to load profile #{profile_name}: #{inspect(e)}")
          {:error, {:render_error, e}}
      end
    else
      {:error, :profile_not_found}
    end
  end
end
