defmodule ACN.Core.Utils.ID do
  @moduledoc """
  Provides utilities for normalizing and slugifying task and node IDs.
  Ensures consistent string keys across the distributed system.
  """

  @doc """
  Normalizes a given ID into a slugified string.
  - Converts atoms to strings.
  - Downcases all characters.
  - Replaces whitespace and non-alphanumeric characters with underscores.
  - Trims balance underscores.
  """
  def normalize(id) when is_atom(id), do: normalize(Atom.to_string(id))
  def normalize(id) when is_binary(id) do
    id
    |> String.replace(~r/([a-z])([A-Z])/, "\\1_\\2")
    |> String.downcase()
    |> String.replace(~r/[^a-z0-9]+/, "_")
    |> String.trim("_")
  end
  def normalize(id), do: normalize(to_string(id))
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: patcher.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule ACN.Core.Utils.Patcher do
  @moduledoc """
  Shared logic for applying Unified Diffs.
  """
  require Logger

  def apply_patch(content, diff) do
    lines = String.split(content, "\n")
    
    # Simple Unified Diff Parser & Applier
    # 1. Parse Hunks
    try do
      hunks = parse_hunks(diff)
      # 2. Apply Hunks
      final_lines = apply_hunks(lines, hunks)
      {:ok, Enum.join(final_lines, "\n")}
    rescue
      e -> {:error, "Patch Logic Error: #{inspect(e)}"}
    end
  end

  defp parse_hunks(diff) do
    diff_lines = String.split(diff, "\n")
    
    # Filter header
    body = Enum.drop_while(diff_lines, fn l -> 
       String.starts_with?(l, "---") or String.starts_with?(l, "+++") or String.starts_with?(l, "Index:") 
    end)
    
    chunk_hunks(body, [])
  end

  defp chunk_hunks([], acc), do: Enum.reverse(acc)
  defp chunk_hunks([head | tail], acc) do
    if String.starts_with?(head, "@@") do
       # Start of new hunk
       {hunk_lines, rest} = Enum.split_while(tail, fn l -> !String.starts_with?(l, "@@") end)
       chunk_hunks(rest, [{head, hunk_lines} | acc])
    else
       # Skip garbage before first hunk?
       chunk_hunks(tail, acc)
    end
  end

  defp apply_hunks(original_lines, hunks) do
    # This is a simplified applier aka "fuzzy patch" or just specific implementation
    # We iterate hunks. For each hunk, we find the "context" in original lines.
    # Note: Unified diff line numbers (in @@) are 1-based.
    
    Enum.reduce(hunks, original_lines, fn {header, changes}, current_lines ->
       # Parse header @@ -Start,Count +Start,Count @@
       # We actually ignore the numbers often in naive patchers and rely on Context Matching
       # because line numbers shift.
       
       # Naive Context Matching:
       # Verify the context lines (starting with ' ') match a block in current_lines.
       # Verify the deleted lines (starting with '-') match the lines inside that block.
       
       # Extract the "Old Block" pattern to find
       pattern = 
         changes 
         |> Enum.filter(fn l -> String.starts_with?(l, " ") or String.starts_with?(l, "-") end)
         |> Enum.map(fn 
              " " <> line -> line
              "-" <> line -> line
              l -> l # Should not happen if well formed
            end)
            
       # Find where this pattern exists in current_lines
       case find_sublist_index(current_lines, pattern) do
         nil -> 
           raise "Hunk context not found. Header: #{header}"
         index ->
           # We found the block at 'index'. Now we reconstruct it using the NEW lines.
           # New lines come from ' ' and '+' in changes.
           replacement_block = 
             changes
             |> Enum.filter(fn l -> String.starts_with?(l, " ") or String.starts_with?(l, "+") end)
             |> Enum.map(fn 
                " " <> line -> line
                "+" <> line -> line
                l -> l 
             end)
             
           # Replace slice
           replace_sublist(current_lines, index, length(pattern), replacement_block)
       end
    end)
  end

  defp find_sublist_index(list, sublist) do
    # Slow naive search
    sub_len = length(sublist)
    limit = length(list) - sub_len
    
    if limit < 0 do
      nil
    else
      Enum.find_index(0..limit, fn i ->
        slice = Enum.slice(list, i, sub_len)
        slice == sublist
      end)
    end
  end

  defp replace_sublist(list, start_index, length_to_remove, new_items) do
    pre = Enum.take(list, start_index)
    post = Enum.drop(list, start_index + length_to_remove)
    pre ++ new_items ++ post
  end
end

------------------------------------------------------------
File: token_authority.ex

defmodule ACN.Core.Security.TokenAuthority do
  use Joken.Config

  @doc "Defines default token configuration"
  def token_config do
    default_claims(iss: "ACN_Controller", aud: "ACN_Actuator")
  end

  @valid_scopes [:fs_write, :fs_read, :exec_cmd, :net_request, :validator, :actuator]

  @doc "Generates a tuple {private_pem_binary, public_pem_binary}"
  def generate_keypair do
    # 1. Generate RSA Key
    private_key = :public_key.generate_key({:rsa, 2048, 65_537})

    # 2. Extract Public Key from Private Key
    {:RSAPrivateKey, _, modulus, public_exponent, _, _, _, _, _, _, _} = private_key
    public_key = {:RSAPublicKey, modulus, public_exponent}

    # 3. Encode both to PEM format
    private_pem = :public_key.pem_encode([:public_key.pem_entry_encode(:RSAPrivateKey, private_key)])
    public_pem = :public_key.pem_encode([:public_key.pem_entry_encode(:RSAPublicKey, public_key)])

    {private_pem, public_pem}
  end

  @doc "Mints a new token with the given scope, resource, and role"
  def mint_token(private_pem, scope, resource, role \\ "agent", ttl \\ 300) do
    if scope not in @valid_scopes, do: raise "Invalid Scope: #{scope}"

    signer = Joken.Signer.create("RS256", %{"pem" => private_pem})

    claims = %{
      "scope" => to_string(scope),
      "resource" => resource,
      "role" => to_string(role),
      "jti" => UUID.uuid4(),
      "exp" => Joken.current_time() + ttl
    }

    generate_and_sign(claims, signer)
  end

  @doc "Verifies a token against the public key, required scope, and resource"
  def verify(token, public_pem, required_scope, requested_resource_args \\ nil) do
    signer = Joken.Signer.create("RS256", %{"pem" => public_pem})

    with {:ok, claims} <- verify_and_validate(token, signer),
         :ok <- validate_scope(claims["scope"], required_scope),
         :ok <- validate_resource(claims["resource"], requested_resource_args) do
      {:ok, claims}
    else
      err -> err
    end
  end

  defp validate_scope(token_scope, required_scope) do
    if token_scope == to_string(required_scope) do
      :ok
    else
      {:error, :invalid_scope}
    end
  end

  defp validate_resource(_token_resource, nil), do: :ok
  defp validate_resource(token_resource, requested_args) do
    # Simple check: Does the token resource match the requested operation's target?
    # For filesystem, args is usually path. For exec, command.
    # The actual matching logic depends on how 'args' are structured.
    # Assuming 'requested_resource' is passed in as the string to check against.

    # If requested_args is a map (tool args), we try to extract common resource keys
    resource_key =
      cond do
        is_binary(requested_args) -> requested_args
        is_map(requested_args) && Map.has_key?(requested_args, :path) -> requested_args[:path]
        is_map(requested_args) && Map.has_key?(requested_args, "path") -> requested_args["path"]
        is_map(requested_args) && Map.has_key?(requested_args, :command) -> requested_args[:command]
        is_map(requested_args) && Map.has_key?(requested_args, "command") -> requested_args["command"]
        is_map(requested_args) && Map.has_key?(requested_args, :dest) -> requested_args[:dest]
        is_map(requested_args) && Map.has_key?(requested_args, "dest") -> requested_args["dest"]
        true -> nil
      end

    cond do
      token_resource == "*" -> :ok # Superuser/Root token (use carefully)
      resource_key == token_resource -> :ok # Exact match
      String.starts_with?(token_resource, "/") and String.starts_with?(resource_key || "", "/") -> 
         # Directory Scope Check: Is resource_key inside token_resource?
         rel = Path.relative_to(resource_key, token_resource)
         if rel != resource_key and not String.starts_with?(rel, "..") do
           :ok
         else
           {:error, :resource_mismatch}
         end
      true -> {:error, :resource_mismatch}
    end
  end
end

defmodule ACN.Core.Artifacts.DBC do
  @moduledoc """
  The Digital Birth Certificate (DBC).
  Strict schema for all permanent Artifacts in the ACN.
  """

  @derive Jason.Encoder
  @enforce_keys [:id, :artifact_hash, :fact_text, :timestamp, :author_id]
  defstruct [
    :id,            # UUID (DBC Unique ID)
    :artifact_hash, # SHA256 of the artifact content
    :fact_text,     # Natural Language Description (The Semantic Anchor)
    :acms_node_id,  # UUID (ACMS Node ID, nil if offline)
    :author_id,     # Agent ID
    :verifier_id,   # Validator ID
    :validation_proof, # Map (Evidence)
    :cost_metrics,  # %{tokens: N, time_ms: T}
    :constituents,  # List of Child DBC IDs (for Intermediate Nodes)
    :timestamp,     # DateTime (Creation Time)
    :provenance_data, # Map (Additional Context)
    :source_code,   # String (The actual implementation content)
    :full_path,     # String (Absolute path to the artifact)
    :filename       # String (Base name of the artifact file)
  ]

  def new(attrs) do
    struct!(__MODULE__, attrs)
  end
end

defmodule ACN.Tools.Communication.DispatchIntent do
  @behaviour ACN.Tools.ToolBehaviour

  def definition do
    %{
      "name" => "dispatch_intent",
      "description" => "Dispatches the classified user intent to the appropriate agent.",
      "parameters" => %{
        "type" => "object",
        "properties" => %{
          "type" => %{
            "type" => "string",
            "enum" => ["query", "command", "garbage"],
            "description" => "Classification of the input type."
          },
          "target" => %{
            "type" => "string",
            "enum" => ["controller", "specialist", "validator", "live_wire", "none"],
            "description" => "The destination agent for this message."
          },
          "intent" => %{
            "type" => "string",
            "description" => "The specific programmatic intention detected."
          },
          "confidence" => %{
            "type" => "number",
            "minimum" => 0.0,
            "maximum" => 1.0,
            "description" => "Probability score (0-1)."
          }
        },
        "required" => ["type", "target", "intent", "confidence"]
      }
    }
  end

  def execute(args, _context) do
    # Logic handled by Messenger Dispatcher
    {:ok, "Intent dispatched to #{args["target"]}."}
  end
end
defmodule ACN.Tools.Communication.SubmitChronicle do
  @behaviour ACN.Tools.ToolBehaviour

  def definition do
    %{
      "name" => "submit_chronicle",
      "description" => "Submits a compressed, human-readable summary of system events.",
      "parameters" => %{
        "type" => "object",
        "properties" => %{
          "summary" => %{
            "type" => "string",
            "description" => "A single line status update (max 100 chars)."
          }
        },
        "required" => ["summary"]
      }
    }
  end

  require Logger
  alias ACN.Core.NodeRegistry

  def execute(args, _context) do
    summary = args["summary"]

    # Resolve Node ID for topic
    node_id =
      case NodeRegistry.lookup(:supervisor) do
        {:ok, %{pid: pid}} ->
           try do
             GenServer.call(pid, :get_node_id, 1000)
           catch
             _, _ -> "root"
           end
        _ -> "root"
      end

    Logger.info("Tool: SubmitChronicle broadcasting: #{summary}")
    Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(ACN.PubSub, "acn:dashboard:#{node_id}", {:narrative_update, summary})

    {:ok, "Chronicle submitted."}
  end
end

File: memory.ex

defmodule ACN.Tools.Memory do
  @moduledoc """
  Tool wrapper for ACN.Infrastructure.MemoryClient.
  Allows Agents to Semantic Search the General Memory (Cognitive Memory System).
  """
  
  defmodule QueryWisdom do
    @behaviour ACN.Tools.ToolBehaviour
    alias ACN.Infrastructure.MemoryClient
    require Logger

    def definition do
      %{
        "name" => "query_wisdom",
        "description" => "Search the General Memory (ACMS) for historical facts, code patterns, or warnings.",
        "parameters" => %{
          "type" => "object",
          "properties" => %{
            "query_text" => %{
              "type" => "string",
              "description" => "The semantic search query (e.g., 'Elixir GenServer best practices')"
            },
            "rationale" => %{
              "type" => "string",
              "description" => "Why this query is relevant to the current task."
            }
          },
          "required" => ["query_text"]
        }
      }
    end

    def execute(args, _env) do
      query = args["query_text"] || args["query"]

      if is_nil(query) or String.trim(query) == "" do
        "Error: 'query_text' argument is required and cannot be empty."
      else
        Logger.debug("Tool Memory: Querying ACMS for '#{query}'")

        case MemoryClient.query(query) do
          {:ok, results} when is_list(results) ->
             if Enum.empty?(results) do
               "No relevant memories found."
             else
               results
               |> Enum.take(5) # Top 5
               |> Enum.map(fn r -> 
                  text = Map.get(r, :fact_text) || Map.get(r, "fact_text") || "Unknown"
                  score = Map.get(r, :score) || Map.get(r, "score") || 0.0
                  "- #{text} (Score: #{Float.round(score, 2)})" 
               end)
               |> Enum.join("\n")
             end
             
          {:error, reason} ->
             "Memory Search Failed: #{inspect(reason)}"
             
          _ -> "Unexpected response from MemoryClient"
        end
      end
    end
  end

  defmodule IngestFact do
    @behaviour ACN.Tools.ToolBehaviour
    alias ACN.Infrastructure.MemoryClient
    require Logger

    def definition do
      %{
        "name" => "ingest_fact",
        "description" => "Immortalize a fact or artifact in the Global Memory (ACMS).",
        "parameters" => %{
          "type" => "object",
          "properties" => %{
            "fact_text" => %{
               "type" => "string",
               "description" => "A descriptive, declarative title or summary of the artifact."
            },
            "interfaces" => %{
               "type" => "array",
               "items" => %{"type" => "string"},
               "description" => "List of public interfaces/functionalities."
            },
            "schema" => %{
               "type" => "object",
               "description" => "Data structure definition."
            }
          },
          "required" => ["fact_text"]
        }
      }
    end

    def execute(args, _env) do
      fact = args["fact_text"]
      
      # Build Real DBC for ingestion
      dbc = %ACN.Core.Artifacts.DBC{
        id: UUID.uuid4(),
        fact_text: fact,
        author_id: "judge_bot_archivist",
        timestamp: DateTime.utc_now(),
        verifier_id: "judge_bot",
        artifact_hash: :crypto.hash(:sha256, fact) |> Base.encode16(),
        provenance_data: %{
          interfaces: args["interfaces"] || [],
          schema: args["schema"] || %{},
          node: node()
        }
      }

      case MemoryClient.ingest(dbc) do
        {:ok, node_id} -> "Fact ingested into ACMS. Node ID: #{node_id}"
        {:error, reason} -> "Ingestion Failed: #{inspect(reason)}"
      end
    end
  end
  defmodule ProbeLattice do
    @behaviour ACN.Tools.ToolBehaviour
    alias ACN.Core.LatticeRegistry
    require Logger

    def definition do
      %{
        "name" => "probe_lattice",
        "description" => "Discover capabilities, modules, and file paths in the ACN. Use this to find dependencies or existing code.",
        "parameters" => %{
          "type" => "object",
          "properties" => %{
            "query" => %{
              "type" => "string",
              "description" => "Search query (e.g. 'Authentication module', 'lib/user/auth/login.ex')"
            }
          },
          "required" => ["query"]
        }
      }
    end

    def execute(args, _env) do
      query = args["query"]
      
      # 1. Try Exact Path Lookup
      path_res = LatticeRegistry.lookup_by_path(query)
      
      # 2. Semantic Search
      search_res = LatticeRegistry.search(query)
      
      # Combine Results
      report = []
      
      report = 
        case path_res do
          {:ok, reg} -> report ++ ["EXACT PATH MATCH: Module #{reg.module} at #{reg.path}"]
          _ -> report
        end
        
      report = 
        case search_res do
          {:ok, results} -> 
            sem_results = Enum.map(results, fn r -> "- Module #{r.module} at #{r.path} (Similarity: #{Float.round(r.score, 2)})" end)
            report ++ ["SEMANTIC MATCHES:"] ++ sem_results
          _ -> report
        end
        
      if Enum.empty?(report) do
        "No matching capabilities found in the Lattice."
      else
        Enum.join(report, "\n")
      end
    end
  end
end

defmodule ACN.Tools.Planning do
  @moduledoc """
  Tools for Graph Manipulation by the TaskManager.
  These tools are designed to be run INSIDE the TaskManager process (process dictionary/self).
  """

  defmodule AddNode do
    @behaviour ACN.Tools.ToolBehaviour
    require Logger

    def definition do
      %{
        "name" => "add_plan_node",
        "description" => "Adds a task node to the execution graph. Use this to decompose the goal.",
        "parameters" => %{
          "type" => "object",
          "properties" => %{
            "name" => %{
              "type" => "string",
              "description" => "Short, unique name for the task (e.g., 'build_database')"
            },
            "description" => %{
              "type" => "string",
              "description" => "Detailed instruction of what this task must achieve."
            },
            "output_directive" => %{
               "type" => "string",
               "description" => "Explicit instruction on what this task must produce (e.g., 'A filepath to the compiled binary', 'The implementation of Foo module'). This dictates the artifact contract."
            },
            "merge_slot_id" => %{
               "type" => "string",
               "description" => "Optional: Unique identifier for the worksheet slot where the child's result will be merged."
            }
          },
          "required" => ["name", "description", "output_directive"]
        }
      }
    end

    def execute(args, _env) do
      # 1. Validate required 'name' field
      uuid = args["name"]
      
      cond do
        is_nil(uuid) ->
          Logger.error("Tool AddNode: REJECTED - Missing 'name' parameter")
          {:error, "Missing required parameter 'name'. Please provide a unique task name."}
        
        String.trim(uuid) == "" ->
          Logger.error("Tool AddNode: REJECTED - Empty 'name' parameter")
          {:error, "Task name cannot be empty. Please provide a valid task name."}
        
        true ->
          # 2. Update GenServer State (via Cast)
          # We assume self() is the TaskManager GenServer
          GenServer.cast(self(), {:add_node, uuid, args})

          Logger.debug("Tool AddNode: Queued #{uuid}")

          {:ok, "Node '#{uuid}' added to plan."}
      end
    end
  end

  defmodule LinkDependency do
    @behaviour ACN.Tools.ToolBehaviour
    require Logger

    def definition do
      %{
        "name" => "link_dependency",
        "description" => "Defines a dependency: Provider must finish BEFORE Consumer starts.",
        "parameters" => %{
          "type" => "object",
          "properties" => %{
            "provider_task_id" => %{
              "type" => "string",
              "description" => "The task that produces data (run first)."
            },
            "consumer_task_id" => %{
              "type" => "string",
              "description" => "The task that needs the data (run second)."
            }
          },
          "required" => ["provider_task_id", "consumer_task_id"]
        }
      }
    end

    def execute(args, _env) do
      provider = args["provider_task_id"]
      consumer = args["consumer_task_id"]

      cond do
        is_nil(provider) or is_nil(consumer) ->
           {:error, "Missing provider or consumer task ID."}
        String.trim(provider) == "" or String.trim(consumer) == "" ->
           {:error, "Provider or consumer task ID cannot be empty."}
        provider == consumer ->
           {:error, "Cannot link task to itself (Cycle detected)."}
        true ->
           GenServer.cast(self(), {:add_dependency, provider, consumer})
           Logger.debug("Tool LinkDependency: Queued #{provider} -> #{consumer}")
            {:ok, "Dependency linked: #{provider} -> #{consumer}"}
      end
    end
  end

  defmodule SubmitExecutionPlan do
    @behaviour ACN.Tools.ToolBehaviour

    def definition do
      %{
        "name" => "submit_execution_plan",
        "description" => "Submits the final parallelized execution plan.",
        "parameters" => %{
          "type" => "object",
          "properties" => %{
            "batches" => %{
              "type" => "array",
              "items" => %{
                "type" => "array",
                "items" => %{
                  "type" => "object",
                  "properties" => %{
                    "id" => %{"type" => "string"}
                  },
                  "required" => ["id"]
                }
              },
              "description" => "A list of batches, where each batch is a list of task IDs that can run in parallel."
            }
          },
          "required" => ["batches"]
        }
      }
    end

    def execute(args, _context) do
      # Logic handled by TaskManager
      {:ok, "Execution plan submitted: #{length(args["batches"] || [])} batches."}
    end
  end
end

defmodule ACN.Tools.Registry do
  @moduledoc """
  Maps string tool names (from YAML profiles) to Elixir Modules.
  Serves as the central directory for all available capabilities.
  """
  
  # Static mapping of tool names to modules
  # As we refactor, we will populate this map
  @tools %{
    "fs_write" => ACN.Tools.FileSystem.Write,
    "read_file" => ACN.Tools.FileSystem.Read,
    "add_plan_node" => ACN.Tools.Planning.AddNode,
    "link_dependency" => ACN.Tools.Planning.LinkDependency,
    "upstream_merge" => ACN.Tools.Lattice.UpstreamMerge,
    "submit_fact" => ACN.Tools.Lattice.SubmitFact,
    
    # Memory Tools
    "query_wisdom" => ACN.Tools.Memory.QueryWisdom,
    # "ingest_fact" => ACN.Tools.Memory.IngestFact,
    "set_node_identity" => ACN.Tools.Supervisor.Identity,
    "submit_legislation" => ACN.Tools.Governance.SubmitLegislation,
    "submit_verdict" => ACN.Tools.Governance.SubmitVerdict,
    "submit_mediation" => ACN.Tools.Governance.SubmitMediation,
    "submit_execution_plan" => ACN.Tools.Planning.SubmitExecutionPlan,

    "dispatch_intent" => ACN.Tools.Communication.DispatchIntent,
    "submit_blueprint" => ACN.Tools.Governance.SubmitBlueprint,

    "write_to_file" => ACN.Tools.FileSystem.Write,
    "touch" => ACN.Tools.FileSystem.Touch,
    "mkdir" => ACN.Tools.FileSystem.Mkdir,
    "submit_plan" => ACN.Tools.Planning.SubmitPlan,
    "submit_chronicle" => ACN.Tools.Communication.SubmitChronicle,
    "submit_strategy" => ACN.Tools.Strategy.SubmitStrategy
  }

  @doc """
  Resolves a list of tool names to their implementation modules.
  Raises if a tool name is unknown (Fail Fast).
  """
  @spec lookup_all(list(String.t())) :: list(module())
  def lookup_all(names) do
    Enum.map(names, &lookup!/1)
  end

  @doc """
  Resolves a single tool name to its module.
  """
  @spec lookup!(String.t()) :: module()
  def lookup!(name) do
    case Map.fetch(@tools, name) do
      {:ok, module} -> module
      :error -> raise "CRITICAL: Tool '#{name}' requested but not registered in ACN.Tools.Registry!"
    end
  end
  
  # For dynamic registration later if needed (Phase 2+)
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: lattice.ex

defmodule ACN.Tools.Lattice do
  @moduledoc """
  Tools for Distributed Lattice Communication (Parent-Child).
  """

  defmodule UpstreamMerge do
    @behaviour ACN.Tools.ToolBehaviour
    require Logger
    alias ACN.Core.NodeRegistry

    alias ACN.Core.Artifacts.DBC

    def definition do
      %{
        "name" => "upstream_merge",
        "description" => "Builds and pushes a finalized Digital Birth Certificate (DBC) including technical summaries and source code to the Parent Node and ACMS.",
        "parameters" => %{
          "type" => "object",
          "properties" => %{
            "fact_text" => %{
              "type" => "string",
              "description" => "A high-level natural language summary of what this artifact does."
            },
            "utility" => %{
              "type" => "string",
              "description" => "Description of the specific utility or value this artifact provides."
            },
            "behaviour" => %{
              "type" => "string",
              "description" => "Description of the expected behavior, state changes, or side effects."
            },
            "interfaces" => %{
              "type" => "array",
              "items" => %{"type" => "string"},
              "description" => "List of public functions, parameters, and return types."
            },
            "schema" => %{
              "type" => "object",
              "description" => "Data structure, types, or configuration used by this artifact."
            },
            "source_code" => %{
              "type" => "string",
              "description" => "The final, verified source code content."
            },
            "slot_id" => %{
              "type" => "string",
              "description" => "The specific memory slot ID in the Parent (usually the task ID)."
            },
            "parent_id" => %{
              "type" => "string",
              "description" => "The node ID of the Parent Supervisor. Always use the parent's exact string name (e.g., 'db_models'), never a UUID."
            },
            "artifact_hash" => %{
               "type" => "string",
               "description" => "Optional SHA256 hash of the artifact content."
            },
            "full_path" => %{
               "type" => "string",
               "description" => "The absolute path to the written file."
            },
            "filename" => %{
               "type" => "string",
               "description" => "The basename of the written file."
            }
          },
          "required" => ["fact_text", "slot_id", "parent_id", "source_code"]
        }
      }
    end

    def execute(args, env) do
      slot_id = args["slot_id"]
      parent_id = args["parent_id"]
      source = args["source_code"] || ""
      
      # 1. Build DBC Struct
      dbc = %DBC{
        id: UUID.uuid4(),
        fact_text: args["fact_text"],
        timestamp: DateTime.utc_now(),
        author_id: "agent_node_#{node()}",
        verifier_id: "judge_bot",
        artifact_hash: args["artifact_hash"] || (:crypto.hash(:sha256, source) |> Base.encode16()),
        source_code: source,
        full_path: args["full_path"],
        filename: args["filename"],
        provenance_data: %{
          interfaces: args["interfaces"] || [],
          schema: args["schema"] || %{},
          utility: args["utility"] || "N/A",
          behaviour: args["behaviour"] || "N/A",
          node: node()
        }
      }

      Logger.info("Tool Lattice: Pushing DBC to Parent #{parent_id} (Slot: #{slot_id}).")

      # 2. Resolve Parent and Push to Worksheet (Parent will handle ACMS ingestion)
      lookup_result = NodeRegistry.lookup(parent_id)

      case lookup_result do
        {:ok, %{pid: pid}} ->
          push_to_parent(pid, slot_id, dbc)
        _ -> 
           local_node_id = Map.get(env, :node_id, "root")
           
           if local_node_id == "root" and to_string(parent_id) in ["nonode@nohost", "", "nil", "root"] do
             # It's the root node, there is no upstream. 
             {:ok, dbc}
           else
             Logger.warning("UpstreamMerge: Could not find node #{parent_id}. Attempting local node fallback.")
             case NodeRegistry.lookup(local_node_id) do
               {:ok, %{pid: pid}} ->
                 push_to_parent(pid, slot_id, dbc)
               _ ->
                  # Last ditch: try "root"
                  case NodeRegistry.lookup("root") do
                    {:ok, %{pid: pid}} -> push_to_parent(pid, slot_id, dbc)
                    _ -> {:error, "Parent #{parent_id} Not Found and local fallback failed."}
                  end
             end
           end
      end
    end

    defp push_to_parent(pid, slot_id, dbc) do
      try do
        case GenServer.call(pid, {:receive_child_artifact, slot_id, dbc, slot_id}, 15_000) do
          :ok -> {:ok, dbc}
          {:error, reason} -> {:error, "Parent Rejected: #{inspect(reason)}"}
          other -> {:ok, other} # Handle varied returns
        end
      catch
        :exit, reason -> {:error, "Parent Unreachable (EXIT): #{inspect(reason)}"}
        kind, err -> {:error, "Parent Unreachable (#{kind}): #{inspect(err)}"}
      end
    end
  end

  defmodule SubmitFact do
    @behaviour ACN.Tools.ToolBehaviour

    def definition do
      %{
        "name" => "submit_fact",
        "description" => "Submits a semantic fact (declarative sentence) about a task result.",
        "parameters" => %{
          "type" => "object",
          "properties" => %{
            "fact" => %{
              "type" => "string",
              "description" => "A single, declarative sentence describing the function and intent of the artifact."
            }
          },
          "required" => ["fact"]
        }
      }
    end

    def execute(_args, _context) do
      {:ok, "Fact submitted."}
    end
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: tool_behaviour.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule ACN.Tools.ToolBehaviour do
  @moduledoc """
  Defines the contract for all Agent Tools.
  Every tool exposed to the LLM must implement this behaviour.
  """

  @doc """
  Returns the JSON Schema definition for the tool.
  Format must be compatible with OpenAI function calling.
  Example:
  %{
    "name" => "fs_write",
    "description" => "Writes text to a file",
    "parameters" => %{
      "type" => "object",
      "properties" => %{...},
      "required" => ["path", "content"]
    }
  }
  """
  @callback definition() :: map()

  @doc """
  Executes the tool with the given arguments.
  
  Args:
  - args: Map of arguments (parsed from JSON).
  
  Returns:
  - {:ok, result_string} : Successful execution output (to be fed back to LLM).
  - {:error, reason_string} : Failure message (to be fed back to LLM).
  """
  @callback execute(args :: map(), env :: map()) :: {:ok, String.t()} | {:error, String.t()}
end

defmodule ACN.Tools.Governance.SubmitLegislation do
  @moduledoc """
  Tool for the Legislator to submit the synthesized RuleSheet.
  """
  @behaviour ACN.Tools.ToolBehaviour
  require Logger

  @impl true
  def definition do
    %{
      "name" => "submit_legislation",
      "description" => "Submits the final synthesized RuleSheet (list of requirements and constraints).",
      "parameters" => %{
        "type" => "object",
        "properties" => %{
          "rules" => %{
            "type" => "array",
            "items" => %{"type" => "string"},
            "description" => "A list of strings defining strict functional requirements and constraints."
          }
        },
        "required" => ["rules"]
      }
    }
  end

  @impl true
  def execute(args, _context) do
    rules = args["rules"] || []
    if rules == [], do: Logger.warning("SubmitLegislation: Received EMPTY rules list!")
    {:ok, "Legislation submitted: #{length(rules)} rules generated."}
  end
end

defmodule ACN.Tools.FileSystem.Touch do
  @moduledoc """
  Exposes the 'touch' capability to agents.
  """
  @behaviour ACN.Tools.ToolBehaviour

  require Logger
  alias ACN.Infrastructure.Workspace

  @impl true
  def definition do
    %{
      name: "touch",
      description: "Creates a file at the specified path (touch).",
      parameters: %{
        type: "object",
        properties: %{
          path: %{
            type: "string",
            description: "The absolute path to the file to create."
          }
        },
        required: ["path"]
      }
    }
  end

  @impl true
  def execute(args, env) do
    path = args["path"] || args[:path]
    node_id = Map.get(env, :node_id, "root")
    working_path = Map.get(env, :working_path)

    Logger.info("Tool [touch] executed for #{path}")

    # Implicitly ensure directory exists to prevent 'enoent' for the user
    # This is a safe helper behavior for a "smart" touch.
    Path.dirname(path) |> File.mkdir_p!()

    case Workspace.touch(node_id, path, working_path) do
      {:ok, abs_path} -> 
        {:ok, "Successfully touched #{abs_path}"}
      {:error, reason} -> 
        {:error, "Failed to touch file: #{inspect(reason)}"}
    end
  end
end

end

defmodule ACN.Tools.FileSystem.Write do
  @moduledoc """
  Exposes file writing capabilities to agents.
  """
  @behaviour ACN.Tools.ToolBehaviour

  require Logger
  alias ACN.Infrastructure.Workspace

  @impl true
  def definition do
    %{
      name: "write_to_file",
      description: "Writes content to a file at the specified path. Supports creating new files or overwriting existing ones.",
      parameters: %{
        type: "object",
        properties: %{
          path: %{
            type: "string",
            description: "The absolute path to the file to write."
          },
          content: %{
            type: "string",
            description: "The text content to write to the file."
          },
          mode: %{
            type: "string",
            enum: ["overwrite", "append"],
            description: "Mode operation. Default is overwrite."
          }
        },
        required: ["path", "content"]
      }
    }
  end

  @impl true
  def execute(args, context) do
    path = args["path"] || args[:path]
    content = args["content"] || args[:content]
    mode = args["mode"] || args[:mode] || "overwrite"

    # Resolve node_id from the calling node's context (injected by Actuator).
    # Falling back to "root" is the last resort — if this happens in production,
    # it means the Actuator forgot to set the env before running the Pipeline.
    node_id = context[:node_id] || context["node_id"] || "root"

    if node_id == "root" do
      Logger.warning("Tool [write_to_file] fallback to 'root' node_id. Actuator may not have injected context.")
    end

    Logger.info("Tool [write_to_file] executed for #{path} (node: #{node_id})")

    case mode do
      "append" -> Workspace.append(node_id, path, content)
      _ -> Workspace.write_atomic(node_id, path, content)
    end
    |> case do
      {:ok, abs_path} -> 
        {:ok, "Successfully wrote to #{abs_path}"}
      {:error, reason} -> 
        {:error, "Failed to write file: #{inspect(reason)}"}
    end
  end
end

defmodule ACN.Tools.FileSystem.Mkdir do
  @behaviour ACN.Tools.ToolBehaviour

  def definition do
    %{
      "name" => "mkdir",
      "description" => "Creates a directory at the given path, including parent directories if they don't exist.",
      "parameters" => %{
        "type" => "object",
        "properties" => %{
          "path" => %{
            "type" => "string",
            "description" => "The absolute path of the directory to create."
          }
        },
        "required" => ["path"]
      }
    }
  end

  def execute(args, env) do
    path = args["path"]
    node_id = Map.get(env, :node_id, "root")
    working_path = Map.get(env, :working_path)
    
    case ACN.Infrastructure.Workspace.safe_path(node_id, path, working_path) do
      {:ok, safe_path} -> 
        case File.mkdir_p(safe_path) do
          :ok -> "Directory created: #{safe_path}"
          {:error, reason} -> "Failed to create directory: #{inspect(reason)}"
        end
      {:error, reason} ->
         "Failed to create directory (Spatial Violation): #{inspect(reason)}"
    end
  end
end

defmodule ACN.Tools.Planning.SubmitPlan do
  @behaviour ACN.Tools.ToolBehaviour

  def definition do
    %{
      "name" => "submit_plan",
      "description" => "Submit the batched execution plan.",
      "parameters" => %{
        "type" => "object",
        "properties" => %{
          "batches" => %{
            "type" => "array",
            "description" => "List of batches, where each batch is a list of task objects.",
            "items" => %{
              "type" => "array",
              "items" => %{
                "type" => "object",
                  "properties" => %{
                    "id" => %{"type" => "string"},
                    "description" => %{"type" => "string"},
                    "output_slot" => %{"type" => "string"},
                    "path" => %{"type" => "string", "description" => "Absolute path where the task is performed"}
                  },
                  "required" => ["id", "description", "output_slot", "path"]
              }
            }
          }
        },
        "required" => ["batches"]
      }
    }
  end

  def execute(args, _env) do
    # Just return the data, the Agent handles it.
    args["batches"]
  end
end

defmodule ACN.Tools.Supervisor.Identity do
  @behaviour ACN.Tools.ToolBehaviour
  require Logger

  def definition do
    %{
      "name" => "set_node_identity",
      "description" => "Sets the identity of the node (LEAF or INTERMEDIATE).",
      "parameters" => %{
        "type" => "object",
        "properties" => %{
          "identity" => %{
             "type" => "string",
             "enum" => ["LEAF", "INTERMEDIATE"],
             "description" => "The determined identity based on task complexity."
          },
          "path" => %{
             "type" => "string",
             "description" => "Optional: The absolute path for the node's workspace (used primarily by root nodes)."
          }
        },
        "required" => ["identity"]
      }
    }
  end

  def execute(params, _env) do
    identity_str = params["identity"]
    path = params["path"]

    identity = 
      case String.upcase(identity_str) do
        "LEAF" -> :leaf
        "INTERMEDIATE" -> :intermediate
        _ -> :leaf # Default safety
      end
      
    Logger.warning("🔧 Tool executing: Setting Node Identity to #{inspect(identity)} (path: #{inspect(path)})")
    
    # Cast back to the Agent to update state
    GenServer.cast(self(), {:set_identity, identity, path})
    
    {:ok, "Identity set to #{identity_str}. Proceeding to Morphic State transition."}
  end
end

File: tokenizer.ex

defmodule ACN.AI.Tokenizer do
  @moduledoc """
  Service for counting tokens in text and messages.
  Currently uses an advanced heuristic fallback (Character Count / 3.5).
  """

  @doc """
  Counts tokens in a string.
  Heuristic: 1 token ~= 3.5 - 4 chars. We use 3.5 to be safe (over-estimate slightly).
  """
  @spec count_tokens(any()) :: integer()
  def count_tokens(nil), do: 0
  def count_tokens(text) when not is_binary(text) do
    # Fallback for non-string types (e.g. lists, maps injected by accident)
    count_tokens(inspect(text))
  end
  def count_tokens(text) do
    case ACN.Actuator.RustlerBridge.count_tokens_nif(text) do
      {:ok, count} -> count
      {:error, reason} -> 
        require Logger
        Logger.error("Tokenizer NIF Failed: #{inspect(reason)}")
        (String.length(text) / 3.5) |> ceil()
      _ -> 0
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Counts tokens in a list of message maps.
  Accounts for message overhead (<|im_start|>, roles, etc).
  """
  @spec count_message_list(list(map())) :: integer()
  def count_message_list(messages) do
    # Overhead per message (format wrappers)
    # Approx 3 tokens per message for formatting
    per_message_overhead = 3
    
    Enum.reduce(messages, 0, fn msg, acc ->
      content_tokens = count_tokens(msg[:content])
      
      # Count tool calls if present (json payload)
      tool_tokens = 
        if msg[:tool_calls] do
          count_tokens(Jason.encode!(msg[:tool_calls])) 
        else
          0
        end

      acc + content_tokens + tool_tokens + per_message_overhead
    end)
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: model_behavior.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule ACN.AI.ModelBehavior do
  @moduledoc """
  Defines the contract for an Intelligence Provider (LLM Adapter).
  Any module implementing this behavior can plug into the Cognitive Loop.
  """

  defmodule Response do
    @type t :: %__MODULE__{
      content: String.t() | nil,
      thinking: String.t() | nil,
      tool_calls: list(map()) | nil,
      token_usage: map() | nil,
      finish_reason: String.t() | nil
    }
    defstruct [:content, :thinking, :tool_calls, :token_usage, :finish_reason]
  end

  @type message :: %{
    role: String.t(), # "user" | "system" | "assistant" | "tool"
    content: String.t() | nil,
    tool_calls: list(map()) | nil, # Optional, for assistant messages
    tool_call_id: String.t() | nil # Optional, for tool messages
  }

  @type tool_definition :: map() # JSON Schema map
  @type options :: keyword()

  @doc """
  Generates a chat completion.
  
  Args:
  - messages: List of standardized message maps.
  - tools: List of JSON Schema definitions for available tools.
  - opts: Configuration overrides (e.g., timeout, temperature).
  
  Returns:
  - {:ok, Response.t()} on success.
  - {:error, reason} on failure (after retries).
  """
  @callback chat(messages :: list(message()), tools :: list(tool_definition()), opts :: options()) ::
              {:ok, Response.t()} | {:error, any()}

  @doc """
  Generates cognitive embeddings for a given text.
  
  Args:
  - text: The input string.
  - opts: Configuration overrides.
  
  Returns:
  - {:ok, list(float())} on success.
  - {:error, any()} on failure.
  """
  @callback embed(text :: String.t(), opts :: options()) ::
              {:ok, list(float())} | {:error, any()}
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: context_buffer.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule ACN.AI.ContextBuffer do
  @moduledoc """
  Manages the sliding window context for the LLM.
  ensures total tokens never exceed max_tokens - safety_margin.
  """
  alias ACN.AI.Tokenizer

  defmodule State do
    defstruct [:system_prompt, :message_history, :max_context_size, :safety_margin]
  end

  @default_max_context 128_000 # Large Context Window
  @default_safety_margin 4_096 # Reserve output space

  def new(system_prompt, opts \\ []) do
    max_context = opts[:max_context_size] || @default_max_context
    safety_margin = opts[:safety_margin] || @default_safety_margin

    %State{
      system_prompt: %{role: "system", content: system_prompt},
      message_history: [],
      max_context_size: max_context,
      safety_margin: safety_margin
    }
  end

  def add_message(%State{} = state, message) do
    # Append message
    new_history = state.message_history ++ [message]

    # Prune immediately to enforce limits
    prune_history(%{state | message_history: new_history})
  end

  def add_messages(%State{} = state, messages) do
    new_history = state.message_history ++ messages
    prune_history(%{state | message_history: new_history})
  end

  def add_tool_result(%State{} = state, tool_call_id, content) do
    message = %{
      role: "tool",
      tool_call_id: tool_call_id,
      content: content
    }
    add_message(state, message)
  end

  def to_list(%State{} = state) do
    [state.system_prompt | state.message_history]
  end

  # --- Pruning Logic ---

  defp prune_history(state) do
    current_tokens = total_tokens(state)
    limit = state.max_context_size - state.safety_margin

    if current_tokens > limit do
      # Drop oldest message (from the front), but keep system prompt safe in struct
      case state.message_history do
        [] ->
          # Crisis: Even system prompt + 0 messages is too big?
          # We can't prune system prompt logic here (Priority 0).
          # Just return state and hope for best or crash?
          state

        [_dropped | remaining] ->
          # Recursively prune until fit
          prune_history(%{state | message_history: remaining})
      end
    else
      state
    end
  end

  defp total_tokens(state) do
    Tokenizer.count_tokens(state.system_prompt.content) +
    Tokenizer.count_message_list(state.message_history)
  end
end

File: pipeline.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule ACN.AI.Pipeline do
  @moduledoc """
  The Cognitive Loop (ReAct Engine).
  Orchestrates the flow: Prompt -> LLM -> Tool -> Execution -> Result -> LLM.
  """
  require Logger
  alias ACN.AI.{ContextBuffer, ModelBehavior}
  alias ACN.Tools.Registry



  @doc """
  Runs the cognitive loop.

  Args:
  - buffer: ACN.AI.ContextBuffer struct
  - tools: List of tool names (strings) available to the agent
  - config: Map containing :adapter_module and :adapter_opts

  Returns:
  - {:ok, final_message, updated_buffer, updated_env}
  - {:error, reason}
  """
  def run(buffer, tool_names, config, env \\ %{}) do
    # 1. Resolve Tool Definitions
    tool_modules = Registry.lookup_all(tool_names)
    tool_definitions = Enum.map(tool_modules, & &1.definition())
 
    # 2. Start Execution
    attempts = config[:max_attempts] || 3
    do_run(buffer, tool_modules, tool_definitions, config, env, attempts)
  end


  defp do_run(buffer, tool_modules, tool_definitions, config, env, attempts) when attempts > 0 do
    adapter = config[:adapter] || ACN.AI.Adapters.OllamaAdapter
    opts = config[:opts] || []

    # 3. Call AI
    messages = ContextBuffer.to_list(buffer)

    case adapter.chat(messages, tool_definitions, opts) do
      {:ok, response} ->
        # AUDIT: Log the interaction
        # We need node_id, assuming it's in config or env?
        # For now, let's use a placeholder or extract from env if available
        node_id = env[:node_id] || "unknown_node"
        bot_name = config[:bot_name] || "unknown_bot"
        
        ACN.Observability.AuditLogger.log_bot_io(
          node_id, 
          bot_name, 
          "AI_EXECUTION", 
          messages, 
          response.content || "TOOL_CALL"
        )

        # Tool Call Enforcement: Rerun if tool_choice is ANY but no tool was called
        tool_choice = opts[:tool_choice] || config[:tool_choice] || profile_tool_choice(config)
        force_tool = config[:force_tool] || (tool_choice == "ANY")
        has_tool_calls = response.tool_calls != nil && length(response.tool_calls) > 0

        if force_tool && !has_tool_calls do
          Logger.warning("[Pipeline] Tool call REQUIRED but missing. Retrying (Attempts left: #{attempts - 1})...")
          
          # Inject nudge to force tool use
          nudge = %{role: "user", content: "CRITICAL: You MUST call one of the available tools. Do not just provide a text response."}
          new_buffer = ContextBuffer.add_message(buffer, nudge)
          
          do_run(new_buffer, tool_modules, tool_definitions, config, env, attempts - 1)
        else
          handle_response(response, buffer, tool_modules, tool_definitions, config, env)
        end
 
      {:error, reason} ->
        Logger.warning("[Pipeline] AI Error: #{inspect(reason)}. Retrying (Attempts left: #{attempts - 1})...")
        do_run(buffer, tool_modules, tool_definitions, config, env, attempts - 1)
    end
  end

  defp do_run(_buffer, _tool_modules, _tool_definitions, _config, _env, 0) do
    Logger.error("[Pipeline] Max attempts reached. Failing.")
    {:error, :max_attempts_reached}
  end

  defp profile_tool_choice(config) do
    # Helpers to extract from nested profile structures if available
    if config[:profile] && is_map(config[:profile]) do
       config[:profile]["tool_choice"]
    else
       nil
    end
  end

  defp handle_response(%ModelBehavior.Response{tool_calls: nil} = response, buffer, _, _, _, env) do
    # 4a. Simple Text Response -> Done
    Logger.info("[Pipeline] AI returned simple text: #{String.slice(response.content || "", 0, 100)}...")
    final_msg = %{role: "assistant", content: response.content}
    new_buffer = ContextBuffer.add_message(buffer, final_msg)
    {:ok, final_msg, new_buffer, env}
  end

  defp handle_response(%ModelBehavior.Response{tool_calls: []} = response, buffer, _, _, _, env) do
      # Empty list same as nil
      final_msg = %{role: "assistant", content: response.content}
      new_buffer = ContextBuffer.add_message(buffer, final_msg)
      {:ok, final_msg, new_buffer, env}
  end

  defp handle_response(%ModelBehavior.Response{tool_calls: tool_calls} = response, buffer, modules, _defs, _config, env) do
    # 4b. Tool Calls detected -> Execute and Recurse

    # First, append the Assistant's "Call" message to history
    assistant_msg = %{
      role: "assistant",
      content: response.content || "",
      tool_calls: tool_calls
    }
    buffer_with_call = ContextBuffer.add_message(buffer, assistant_msg)

    # Execute Tools (Sequential for now, could be parallel)
    {tool_results, updated_env} =
      Enum.reduce(tool_calls, {[], env}, fn call, {results, curr_env} ->
        {res, next_env} = execute_tool(call, modules, curr_env)
        {results ++ [res], next_env}
      end)

    # Append Tool Outputs to history
    buffer_with_results = ContextBuffer.add_messages(buffer_with_call, tool_results)

    # RE-ACT LOOP REMOVED as per architectural directive.
    # We return the actual assistant message that triggered the tools
    # so the caller can extract the results or proceed to the next stage.
    {:ok, assistant_msg, buffer_with_results, updated_env}
  end

  defp execute_tool(%{"function" => func, "id" => call_id}, modules, env) do
    tool_name = func["name"]
    args_json = func["arguments"]

    # Find module matching definition name
    # We have list of modules. We need to find the one where def.name == tool_name
    # Optimization: Create a map lookup in `run/3`?
    # For now, simplistic iteration over modules list (assuming small count)

    module = Enum.find(modules, fn mod ->
      mod.definition()["name"] == tool_name
    end)

    {output, updated_env} =
      if module do
        case Jason.decode(args_json) do
          {:ok, args} ->
            try do
              # Determine calling convention (args or args+env)
              case module.execute(args, env) do
                {:ok, result, next_env} -> {result, next_env}
                {:ok, result} -> {result, env}
                {:error, reason} -> {"Error: #{reason}", env}
                other -> {other, env}
              end
            rescue
              e -> {"Error executing tool: #{inspect(e)}", env}
            end
          {:error, _} -> {"Error: Invalid JSON arguments", env}
        end
      else
        {"Error: Tool #{tool_name} not found in registry subset.", env}
      end
 
    # Format as Tool Message
    message = %{
      role: "tool",
      tool_call_id: call_id,
      content: output
    }
    {message, updated_env}
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: embedder.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule ACN.AI.Embedder do
  @moduledoc """
  Public API for Text Embeddings.
  """
  require Logger

  @doc """
  Generates a 384-dimensional cognitive encoding (tensor) from text.
  
  NOTE: This is a local utility for edge-case cognitive processing. 
  Primary Associative Memory (ACMS) offloads encoding to the server side.
  """
  def embed(text) when is_binary(text) do
    if Process.whereis(ACN.Embedder) do
      try_embed_local(text)
    else
      mock_embed(text)
    end
  end

  defp try_embed_local(text) do
     try do
       output = Nx.Serving.batched_run(ACN.Embedder, text)
       tensor = output.embedding
       encoding = Nx.to_flat_list(tensor)
       384 = length(encoding)
       {:ok, encoding}
     rescue
       e ->
         Logger.error("Local Embedding Failed: #{inspect(e)}")
         mock_embed(text)
     end
  end

  defp mock_embed(text) do
    # Generate a deterministic 384-dim vector based on the string hash
    # This allows basic associative logic to work even without a real model.
    seed = :erlang.phash2(text)
    pseudo_random_vector(seed, 384)
  end

  defp pseudo_random_vector(seed, size) do
    # Simple deterministic vector generation (normalized -1.0 to 1.0)
    # This is better than all-zeros as it preserves some "uniqueness" for the text.
    vector = 
      for i <- 1..size do
        (:erlang.phash2({seed, i}) / 4_294_967_296 * 2.0) - 1.0
      end
    {:ok, vector}
  end
end
